  <style>
    :root {
      --neon-purple: #a855f7;
      --gold: #f59e0b;
      --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
      --glass-bg: rgba(15,15,26,0.85);
      --glass-border: rgba(168,85,247,0.38);
      --accent-glow: 0 0 42px 8px #a855f799;
      --text-main: #f9fafb;
      --text-dim: #cfcfe7;
      --radius: 28px;
      --blur: 18px;
      --danger: #ef4444;
      --success: #22c55e;
      --shadow-glass: 0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.82);
      --transition: 0.15s cubic-bezier(.8,.23,.29,.87);
    }
    html,body {height:100%;margin:0;padding:0;}
    body {
      font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 10% 20%, #a855f733 0%, transparent 60%),
                  radial-gradient(circle at 88% 90%, #f59e0b33 0%, transparent 65%),
                  var(--deep-dark);
      color: var(--text-main);
      min-height: 100vh;
      display:flex;align-items:stretch;justify-content:center;
      overflow-x:hidden;
    }
    body::after {
      content:"";
      pointer-events:none;
      position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:0;
      opacity:0.5;
      background: repeating-conic-gradient(from 190deg, #a855f7aa 0deg 12deg, transparent 20deg 36deg, #f59e0baa 36deg 48deg, transparent 60deg 90deg);
      filter:blur(90px);
      mix-blend-mode:screen;
    }
    .app-shell {
      z-index: 1; width:100%; max-width:530px; min-height:97vh;
      display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;align-items:stretch;
      padding:20px 10px 22px;
      position:relative;
    }
    .app-inner {
      background: var(--glass-bg);
      border-radius: 36px;
      border: 1.6px solid var(--glass-border);
      box-shadow: var(--shadow-glass);
      backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur));
      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur));
      overflow:hidden;
      width:100%; flex:1; display:flex;flex-direction:column;
      min-height:660px;
    }
    header, .section {padding:18px 22px 10px;}
    .brand {
      font-family:"JetBrains Mono",monospace;
      letter-spacing:0.15em;text-transform:uppercase;
      font-size:0.82rem;
      color:var(--gold);
      margin-bottom: .5em;
    }
    .tagline {
      font-size:.82rem;color:var(--text-dim);
      font-weight: 400; margin-bottom:1.1em;
    }
    .streak-chip {
      border-radius:999px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;
      background:radial-gradient(circle,var(--gold) 25%,rgba(0,0,0,0.63) 95%);
      padding:4px 16px;font-size:.9em;font-weight:600;color:var(--deep-dark);
      margin-left:auto;
      box-shadow:0 0 9px 4px #f59e0b33 inset;
    }
    .star-dot{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--gold);margin-right:6px;}
    .section-label {text-transform:uppercase;font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-dim);margin-bottom:8px;letter-spacing:0.12em;}
    .hero-card {padding:18px 22px 24px;margin-bottom:14px;background:linear-gradient(100deg,#a855f71a,#f59e0b17 70%,transparent 100%),rgba(18,16,28,0.86);border-radius:var(--radius);box-shadow:0 10px 48px #0f0f1a28;}
    .hero-title {font-size:1.5em;font-weight:800;line-height:1.22;}
    .hero-accent {color:var(--gold);}
    .hero-sub {margin:6px 0 0;color:var(--text-dim);}
    .hero-cta-row{margin-top:14px;display:flex;gap:12px;}
    .btn-pill{
      appearance:none; border:none; cursor:pointer; font-family:inherit;font-weight:600;
      border-radius:999px; padding:11px 26px;font-size:.97em; margin:0 2px;
      background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--neon-purple),var(--gold)); color:#0e0213;
      box-shadow:0 7px 21px #a855f799;transition:var(--transition);
      position:relative;overflow:hidden;
    }
    .btn-pill.secondary{
      background:rgba(249,250,251,0.13); color:var(--text-main);border:1px solid #fff3;
      box-shadow:none;
    } 
    .btn-pill:active {transform:scale(.97);}
    .section{margin-bottom:18px;}
    .pulse-card{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:18px;align-items:center; background:rgba(0,0,0,.70);padding:12px 14px;border-radius:24px;margin-bottom:12px;}
    .avatar-block{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;}
    .avatar-ring, .avatar-core{
      border-radius:999px;display:block;box-shadow:0 0 14px #a855f7a6;
    }
    .avatar-ring{
      width:82px;height:82px;border:3.5px solid #a855f788;animation:spin 14s linear infinite;}
    .avatar-core{ width:54px;height:54px;margin-top:-68px;background:radial-gradient(circle,#a855f7 55%,#210837);box-shadow:0 0 34px #a855f7c9;}
    .avatar-core.happy{background:radial-gradient(circle,var(--gold),#a21caf);box-shadow:0 0 26px #f59e0bcc;}
    .avatar-core.slump{background:radial-gradient(circle,#7f1d1d,#450a0a);box-shadow:0 0 18px #ef4444dd;}
    @keyframes spin{100%{transform:rotate(360deg);}}
    .pulse-meta{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;}
    .chip{display:inline-block;padding:1.5px 9px;border-radius:999px;background:#36254b27;color:var(--neon-purple);font-size:.82em;font-weight:600;}
    /* Tracker */
    .tracker-card{background:rgba(0,0,0,0.84);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:14px 16px;margin-bottom:10px;}
    .tracker-label{color:var(--text-main);font-size:.82em;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:2px;}
    .progress-shell{width:100%;height:9px;background:#a855f70c;border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:2.5px;overflow:hidden;}
    .progress-fill{height:100%;border-radius:8px;width:0;transition:width 0.33s;}
    .listen-bar{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#a855f7,#b39ddb);}
    .speak-bar{background:linear-gradient(90deg,#f59e0b,#fef08a);}
    /* Capabilities + FAQ + Footer omitted for brevity—see previous section for full styles. */
    /* Add your own responsive and a11y modifiers here as in v1.1 base */
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="app-shell">
    <div class="app-inner">
      <header>
        <div class="brand">NET PET AI · ULTRA 1.1</div>
        <div class="tagline">Phoenix × Comet × Claude: Real coach, not an app.</div>
        <div class="streak-chip"><span class="star-dot"></span><span id="streakNum">0</span> days</div>
      </header>
      <div class="section hero-card">
        <div class="section-label">Your AI accountability engine</div>
        <div class="hero-title">
          Build your <span class="hero-accent">English lifestyle</span><br>45·15·EVERY DAY.
        </div>
        <div class="hero-sub">Tracks, coaches, and never lets you stay a mouse.</div>
        <div class="hero-cta-row">
          <button class="btn-pill" id="cta-log">Log today</button>
          <button class="btn-pill secondary" id="cta-more">What can I do?</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="section pulse-card">
        <div class="avatar-block">
          <div class="avatar-ring"></div>
          <div class="avatar-core" id="avatar"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="pulse-meta">
          <div id="chipMood" class="chip">Mood: Neutral</div>
          <div id="chipGoal" class="chip" style="background:#f59e0b24;color:var(--gold);">Goal: Not complete</div>
          <div id="pulseStatus" style="margin-top:6px;font-size:1em;">Let’s check: tiger or mouse today?</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="section tracker-card">
        <div class="tracker-label"><span>Listening (Fun Input)</span><span id="lblListen">0/45m</span></div>
        <div class="progress-shell"><div class="progress-fill listen-bar" id="barListen"></div></div>
        <div class="tracker-label"><span>Speaking (Shadow)</span><span id="lblSpeak">0/15m</span></div>
        <div class="progress-shell"><div class="progress-fill speak-bar" id="barSpeak"></div></div>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin:10px 0;">
          <button class="btn-pill" style="background:var(--neon-purple);" id="btnListen">+15m Listen</button>
          <button class="btn-pill" style="background:var(--gold);" id="btnSpeak">+5m Speak</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="aiLines" style="font-style:italic;font-size:1em;padding:8px 0 0 8px; color:var(--text-dim);"></div>
      <div class="section" id="capabilities"></div>
      <div class="section" id="faq"></div>
      <footer class="section" style="text-align:right;font-size:.75em;color:var(--text-dim;">GOSPEL 444 · PHOENIX × COMET · CLAUDE READY</footer>
    </div>
  </div>
  <script>
    // == ULTRA EVENT BUS (AI, AUDIT, UX, AGENT READY)
    const Bus = {subs:{},on(e,f){(this.subs[e]=this.subs[e]||[]).push(f);},emit(e,x){(this.subs[e]||[]).forEach(fn=>fn(x));}};
    // == STATE ENGINE (HASHING, DAY ROLLOVER, STORAGE)
    const BASE = {streak:0,listen:0,speak:0,last:today(),mood:"NEUTRAL",goal:false};
    let state=loadState();

    function today() { return new Date().toLocaleDateString(); }
    function saveState() { localStorage.setItem("npAI",JSON.stringify(state)); localStorage.setItem("npAI_hash",btoa(JSON.stringify(state))); }
    function loadState() {
      try {
        const raw=localStorage.getItem("npAI");
        return raw ? {...BASE, ...JSON.parse(raw)} : {...BASE};
      } catch { return {...BASE}; }
    }
    function checkDay() {
      if(state.last !== today()) {
        state.last = today();
        if(state.goal) { state.streak++; state.mood="HAPPY"; } else { state.streak=0; state.mood="TOUGH"; }
        state.listen = 0; state.speak=0; state.goal=false;
        saveState();
      }
    }
    // == AGENT VOICE LIBRARY
    const LINES = {
      GREETING:[ "Let’s break the grammar cage today.", "Every day is a new upgrade. Ready for 45/15?", "Consistency, not intensity: that’s NET.", "It’s not magic. It’s math. 45 plus 15, every day." ],
      TOUGH: [ "No speaking yet? Cannot get fluent staring at a screen!", "No streak. Nothing changes if nothing changes. Try again now.", "Tiger, not mouse. Prove it next. Click +5.", "No goals, just reasons? Time to move." ],
      HAPPY: [ "Momentum: stacking wins. Let’s go bigger.", "A little English, every day. That’s the path.", "Goal met! Tomorrow, we do it again.", "You’re building a lifestyle, not cramming." ]
    };
    // == RENDER ENGINE
    function render() {
      document.getElementById("streakNum").textContent=state.streak;
      document.getElementById("lblListen").textContent=`${state.listen}/45m`;
      document.getElementById("lblSpeak").textContent=`${state.speak}/15m`;
      document.getElementById("barListen").style.width = Math.min(100,state.listen/45*100)+"%";
      document.getElementById("barSpeak").style.width = Math.min(100,state.speak/15*100)+"%";
      document.getElementById("chipMood").textContent="Mood: "+(state.mood=="HAPPY"?"Happy":state.mood=="TOUGH"?"Tough":"Neutral");
      document.getElementById("chipGoal").textContent="Goal: "+(state.goal?"Complete":"Not complete");
      document.getElementById("avatar").className="avatar-core"+(state.mood=="HAPPY"?" happy":state.mood=="TOUGH"?" slump":"");
      document.getElementById("pulseStatus").textContent=
        state.mood=="TOUGH"?"Slump detected. Tomorrow we break the wall.":
        state.mood=="HAPPY"?"Keep it up: 45/15 = real English.":
        "Small steps. Big wins.";
      // AI lines
      document.getElementById("aiLines").textContent = pickLine(state.mood=="TOUGH"?"TOUGH":state.mood=="HAPPY"?"HAPPY":"GREETING")
    }
    function pickLine(mood) {
      const pool = LINES[mood]||LINES['GREETING'];
      return pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)];
    }
    // == CONTROLS
    document.getElementById("btnListen").onclick=function(){
      state.listen += 15;
      if(state.listen>45) state.listen=45;
      evaluate(); saveState(); render();
    };
    document.getElementById("btnSpeak").onclick=function(){
      state.speak += 5;
      if(state.speak>15) state.speak=15;
      evaluate(); saveState(); render();
    };
    function evaluate() {
      if(state.listen>=45 && state.speak>=15) { state.goal=true; state.mood="HAPPY"; }
      else if(state.listen>0 && state.speak==0) { state.mood="TOUGH"; }
      else { state.mood="NEUTRAL"; }
    }
    document.getElementById("cta-log").onclick=function(){
      window.scrollTo({top:document.querySelector('.tracker-card').offsetTop-50,left:0,behavior:"smooth"});
    };
    document.getElementById("cta-more").onclick = function(){
      window.scrollTo({top:document.getElementById("capabilities").offsetTop-30,left:0,behavior:"smooth"});
    };

    // == CAPABILITIES + FAQ
    document.getElementById("capabilities").innerHTML =
    `<div class="section-label">What can NET PET do?</div>
      <div style="display:grid;gap:10px;">
        <div class="chip" style="background:#a855f744;color:#f9fafb;">Coach you brutally when you hide</div>
        <div class="chip" style="background:#f59e0b3a;color:#f59e0b;">Lock in your 45·15 streak, day by day</div>
        <div class="chip" style="background:#f59e0b2f;color:#a855f7;">Remind you robot voice ≠ fluent</div>
        <div class="chip" style="background:#a855f71a;color:#f59e0b;">Never lie: only track you, never judge</div>
      </div>`;

    document.getElementById("faq").innerHTML=
    `<div class="section-label">FAQ</div>
      <div class="chip" style="display:block;margin:7px 0;">What if I miss a day? — You snap back to zero. Consistency beats motivation.</div>
      <div class="chip" style="display:block;margin:7px 0;">What gets tracked? — Listening, Speaking, Streak, Mood. Only.</div>
      <div class="chip" style="display:block;margin:7px 0;">Is this a teacher? — No. This is your daily AI coach, not a class.</div>`;

    // == INIT
    checkDay(); evaluate(); saveState(); render();
    Bus.on("state",()=>{saveState();render();});
  </script>
</body>
</html>



This app combines the best of NET/Phoenix canon, Comet's agent-UX, and Claude’s modular patterns:
Modular event-driven code: All main functions are pluggable and easily extracted/tested.
Day rollovers, audit, agent feedback, and UI/UX are ready for all SaaS, AI, or teaching integrations.
Aesthetic law and agent compliance (glassmorphism, gold/purple, zero blue, branding) are strictly locked at every level.
Copy this to any modern static host—even a browser tab—and you instantly get an agentic, streak-based AI language coach for real-world, scalable SaaS, franchise, or product integration.
Ultra Saiyan complete.

🟣 2000-POINT ANALYSIS: COMET LANDING PAGE FUNCTIONS & REVERSE ENGINEERING SNIPPETS


I. FUNCTIONAL, UX, & EXPERIENCE MAP (500 Points)
1–10: Core Experience Structure
Multi-repeat “The browser that works for you” messaging solidifies a user-centric, agentic theme.perplexity​
“A new browser from Perplexity” instantly sets context, brand, and authority.perplexity​
All central headings/pitch repeat to create deep associative branding—visual rhythm anywhere you scroll.perplexity​
“Watch Film” CTAs recur, using visual anchors to draw video engagement.perplexity​
“Do anything with Comet” anchors power-user, delegation, and agent model throughout.
11–25: AI — Capabilities, Delegation, & Trust
“AI that understands”: context-aware search and summarization, with repeated sample (“How are different news outlets covering this differently?”) — showing real world, not fantasy AI.perplexity​
“AI that organizes”: promises tab management, distraction minimization — the agent actively optimizes your day.perplexity​
“AI that builds”: automation of real tasks (website build, pauses) — code/business logic that can trigger workflows, not just simple input.perplexity​
“AI that emails/creates/shops”: promise of multi-skill workflow orchestration from a single AI assistant—keyboard-to-life class APIs, not just Q&A.perplexity​
“Explore more ways to use Comet”: open architecture, signposting infinite use cases — agentic SaaS mindset.perplexity​
26–50: Productivity Centric Flows
“Browse smarter, faster, and with less friction”: focus on velocity, user empowerment.perplexity​
CTA clusters: “Watch Film", “Explore more”, etc., are modular, always above/below fold—lowers bounce risk.perplexity​
“Your personal assistant” callout: envelops unknown use cases under a “delegate anything” UX.perplexity​
“What can Comet do?” → discovery/expansion module — hooks for API/feature exploration.perplexity​
AI-driven value-prop blocks: labeled by function, not by feature (“AI that understands”, not “Comet can analyze news”).perplexity​
51–125: FAQ as Conversion Layer
“What platforms is Comet available on?” — assurance, device reach.perplexity​
“How do I control privacy settings?” — signals compliance, user empowerment.perplexity​
“Is Comet free?” and install flows — onboarding/incentivization.perplexity​
FAQ blocks replicate, increasing access/scannability for various bounce depths.perplexity​
“Default browser”, privacy, and search engine—platform and user data stack transparency.perplexity​
126–200: Repetitive, Modular Copy Pattern
Reuse of “AI that {does}” greatly compresses onboarding friction: any one section sells whole suite.perplexity​
“Browse with intelligence”: persistent footer/closer to create last impression.perplexity​
UI copy is as universal as possible, lowering translation and drift risk across languages and launches.perplexity​
Use of short, active lines allows for future modular language/vertical swaps.perplexity​
CTA/button/FAQ not only repeats, but positions as micro-interaction for rapid agent/event API injection.perplexity​

301–400: Universal Assistant Event Bus
Central Event Bus:
js
const EventBus = {};
EventBus.subscribers = {};
EventBus.emit = function(ev, payload) {
  (EventBus.subscribers[ev]||[]).forEach(fn=>fn(payload));
};
EventBus.on = function(ev, fn) {
  if(!EventBus.subscribers[ev]) EventBus.subscribers[ev]=[];
  EventBus.subscribers[ev].push(fn);
};

Used to:
Route all user + background AI triggers
Pipe assistant API requests/responses
Animate, update UI panels/components live

🟣 NET ULTRA SAIYAN APP — FULL SOURCE


xml
<!-- index.html -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8"/>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0"/>
  <title>NET Ultra Saiyan App</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="styles.css"/>
</head>
<body>
  <nav id="navbar">
    <span id="logo" class="glass-orb">NET</span>
    <button id="nav-buddies">Buddies</button>
    <button id="nav-lessons">Lessons</button>
    <button id="nav-metrics">Metrics</button>
    <button id="nav-settings">Settings</button>
    <button id="nav-about">About</button>
  </nav>
  <main id="app"></main>
  <footer id="footer"></footer>
  <script>
    /* --- Ultra Saiyan Utilities --- */
    const capitalize = str => str.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + str.slice(1);
    function sanitizeInput(str) {
      const d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = str; return d.innerHTML;
    }
    function hashState(obj) {
      return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(obj))));
    }
    /* --- Canonical State --- */
    let state = {
      version: "V1.0.0",
      buddies: [{id:"n1", name:"Nhan"}, {id:"q1", name:"Quang"}],
      lessons: [{id:"l1", title:"First Lesson", complete:false}],
      session: null,
      metrics: { streak:0, minutes:0 },
      settings: { lang:"en", theme:"dark" }
    };
    function saveState() {
      localStorage.setItem("netUltraState", JSON.stringify(state));
      localStorage.setItem("netUltraHash", hashState(state));
    }
    function loadState() {
      const d = localStorage.getItem("netUltraState");
      if(d) try { Object.assign(state, JSON.parse(d)); } catch(e) {}
    }
    /* --- Agent & Audit Hooks --- */
    function logAction(type, payload) {
      console.log(`[AUDIT] ${type}`, payload);
      // future: send to SaaS/api/agent-bus
    }
    function initAgentBus() {
      window.addEventListener('message', e => {
        if(e.data && e.data.type === "AGENT_EVENT") logAction("agent_bus", e.data);
      });
    }
    /* --- UI Core --- */
    function setMain(html) { document.getElementById("app").innerHTML = html; }
    /* --- Components --- */
    function renderBuddyList() {
      setMain(`<div id="buddy-list">
      <h2 class="section-title">Buddies</h2>
        ${state.buddies.map(b=>
          `<div class="buddy-card glass-bg">${capitalize(b.name)}
             <button onclick="lockBuddy('${b.id}')">Lock</button></div>`
        ).join('')}
      <button onclick="addBuddy()">Add Buddy</button>
      </div>`);
    }
    function lockBuddy(id) {
      state.session = {buddy: id, ts: Date.now()};
      logAction('buddy_locked', {id});
      saveState(); renderBuddyList();
    }
    function addBuddy() {
      const n = prompt("Buddy name:")||""; if(!n) return;
      state.buddies.push({id:Math.random().toString(36).slice(2),name:sanitizeInput(n)});
      saveState(); renderBuddyList();
    }
    function renderLessons() {
      setMain(`<div id="lessons">
        <h2 class="section-title">Lessons</h2>
        ${state.lessons.map(l=>
          `<div class="lesson-card glass-bg">
              ${l.title} - ${l.complete?"✅":"❌"}
              <button onclick="toggleLesson('${l.id}')">Toggle Done</button>
           </div>`
        ).join('')}
        <button onclick="addLesson()">Add Lesson</button>
      </div>`);
    }
    function addLesson() {
      const t = prompt("Lesson title:")||""; if(!t) return;
      state.lessons.push({id:Math.random().toString(36).slice(2),title:sanitizeInput(t),complete:false});
      saveState(); renderLessons();
    }
    function toggleLesson(id) {
      const l = state.lessons.find(l=>l.id===id);
      if(l) l.complete=!l.complete;
      saveState(); renderLessons();
    }
    function renderMetrics() {
      setMain(`<div id="metrics" class="glass-bg">
        <h2 class="section-title">Metrics</h2>
        <div>Streak: <b>${state.metrics.streak}</b></div>
        <div>Minutes: <b>${state.metrics.minutes}</b></div>
        <button onclick="incStreak()">+ Streak</button>
        <button onclick="incMinutes()">+ Minutes</button>
      </div>`);
    }
    function incStreak() { state.metrics.streak++; saveState(); renderMetrics(); }
    function incMinutes() { state.metrics.minutes+=15; saveState(); renderMetrics(); }
    function renderSettings() {
      setMain(`<div id="settings" class="glass-bg">
        <h2>Settings</h2>
        <label>Language:
          <select onchange="updateLang(this.value)">
             <option value="en"${state.settings.lang==="en"?" selected":""}>English</option>
             <option value="vi"${state.settings.lang==="vi"?" selected":""}>Vietnamese</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>Theme:
          <select onchange="updateTheme(this.value)">
             <option value="dark"${state.settings.theme==="dark"?" selected":""}>Dark</option>
             <option value="light"${state.settings.theme==="light"?" selected":""}>Light</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>`);
    }
    function updateLang(l) { state.settings.lang = l; saveState(); renderSettings(); }
    function updateTheme(t) { state.settings.theme = t; document.body.className = t; saveState(); }
    function renderAbout() {
      setMain(`<div id="about" class="glass-bg">
        <h2>About NET Ultra Saiyan</h2>
        <p>The evidence-based, zero-drift, confidence-first language universe for professionals.</p>
        <p><b>Canon Ready.</b> <b>Audit-Ready.</b> <b>Agent-Ready.</b></p>
      </div>`);
    }
    /* --- Navigation Setup --- */
    function setupNav() {
      document.getElementById("nav-buddies").onclick = renderBuddyList;
      document.getElementById("nav-lessons").onclick = renderLessons;
      document.getElementById("nav-metrics").onclick = renderMetrics;
      document.getElementById("nav-settings").onclick = renderSettings;
      document.getElementById("nav-about").onclick = renderAbout;
    }
    /* --- App Render Bootstrap --- */
    function renderApp() {
      renderBuddyList();
    }
    /* --- Initialize --- */
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      loadState();
      setupNav();
      renderApp();
      initAgentBus();
      document.body.className = state.settings.theme;
    });
    /* --- Export: Copy or reuse any function above for modular builds --- */
    // All code above can be split into files as needed: it is modular, ES6-compatible, and ready for build systems.
  </script>
  <!-- Ultra SaaS theme styles could go in styles.css -->
  <style>
    body { background: #0f0f1a; color: #fff; font-family: Inter,sans-serif; margin:0; }
    .glass-bg { background: rgba(30,16,60,0.7); backdrop-filter: blur(12px); border-radius: 16px; margin:12px;padding:16px; }
    .section-title { color: #a855f7; }
    .buddy-card, .lesson-card { margin: 8px 0; padding: 8px 14px; border:1px solid #a855f7; border-radius: 12px; }
    nav { display: flex; gap: 12px; background:linear-gradient(90deg, #a855f7 10%, #f59e0b 90%); padding:12px; align-items: center;}
    .glass-orb { background: rgba(50,10,150,0.45); border-radius: 50%; padding:10px 20px; font-weight: bold; margin-right:10px; font-size: 21px; }
    button { background: #f59e0b; color: #111; border:none; border-radius:8px; margin: 0 3px; padding: 6px 12px; cursor:pointer; }
    button:hover { background:#a855f7; color:#fff; }
    select { margin-left:12px; font-size:1em;}
    #app { max-width: 600px; margin:36px auto; }
  </style>
</body>
</html>

## 1️⃣ Standalone app — `net-pet-v2-ultra.html`

Copy this into a file named **`net-pet-v2-ultra.html`** and open in a browser.

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <title>NET PET AI Coach — Phoenix Fusion v2.0</title>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#0f0f1a" />
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
  <style>
    :root {
      --neon-purple: #a855f7;
      --gold: #f59e0b;
      --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
      --glass-bg: rgba(15,15,26,0.9);
      --glass-border: rgba(168,85,247,0.45);
      --text-main: #f9fafb;
      --text-dim: #c7c7e5;
      --danger: #ef4444;
      --success: #22c55e;
      --radius: 28px;
      --blur: 18px;
      --shadow-glass: 0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.9);
      --transition: 0.16s cubic-bezier(.8,.23,.29,.87);
    }
    * { box-sizing:border-box; margin:0; padding:0; }

    body {
      min-height:100vh;
      font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;
      background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 8% 15%, #a855f733 0%, transparent 55%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 92% 90%, #f59e0b33 0%, transparent 60%),
        var(--deep-dark);
      color:var(--text-main);
      display:flex;
      align-items:stretch;
      justify-content:center;
      padding:16px 10px;
      overflow-x:hidden;
      position:relative;
    }
    body::after {
      content:"";
      position:fixed;
      inset:0;
      pointer-events:none;
      background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 10% 100%, #a855f744 0%, transparent 55%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 90% 0%, #f59e0b3a 0%, transparent 60%);
      mix-blend-mode:screen;
      opacity:0.6;
      filter:blur(80px);
    }

    .app-shell {
      z-index:1;
      width:100%;
      max-width:540px;
      min-height:90vh;
      display:flex;
      align-items:center;
      justify-content:center;
    }
    .app-inner {
      width:100%;
      background:var(--glass-bg);
      border-radius:36px;
      border:1.5px solid var(--glass-border);
      box-shadow:var(--shadow-glass);
      backdrop-filter:blur(var(--blur));
      -webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(var(--blur));
      display:flex;
      flex-direction:column;
      max-height:840px;
      overflow:hidden;
    }
    header, .section { padding:18px 20px 12px; }

    header {
      display:flex;
      align-items:flex-start;
      gap:10px;
      border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);
    }
    .brand {
      font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace;
      font-size:0.8rem;
      text-transform:uppercase;
      letter-spacing:0.16em;
      color:var(--gold);
      margin-bottom:4px;
    }
    .tagline {
      font-size:0.82rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      max-width:220px;
    }
    .streak-chip {
      margin-left:auto;
      display:flex;
      align-items:center;
      gap:6px;
      padding:4px 14px;
      border-radius:999px;
      background:radial-gradient(circle,var(--gold) 30%,rgba(0,0,0,0.8) 100%);
      color:#0b0213;
      font-size:0.82rem;
      font-weight:600;
      box-shadow:0 0 0 1px rgba(249,250,251,0.15), 0 0 16px #f59e0b88 inset;
    }
    .streak-dot { width:10px; height:10px; border-radius:999px; background:var(--gold); }

    .section-label {
      text-transform:uppercase;
      font-size:0.7rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      letter-spacing:0.14em;
      margin-bottom:6px;
    }

    .hero-card {
      padding:18px 20px 22px;
      background:
        linear-gradient(110deg,#a855f71a,#f59e0b16 65%,transparent 100%),
        rgba(7,7,14,0.9);
      margin:10px 16px 4px;
      border-radius:var(--radius);
      box-shadow:0 14px 45px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
    }
    .hero-title {
      font-size:1.5rem;
      font-weight:800;
      line-height:1.2;
    }
    .hero-accent { color:var(--gold); }
    .hero-sub {
      font-size:0.9rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      margin-top:6px;
    }
    .hero-cta-row {
      margin-top:12px;
      display:flex;
      gap:10px;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
    }
    .btn-pill {
      appearance:none;
      border:none;
      cursor:pointer;
      font-family:inherit;
      font-weight:600;
      font-size:0.9rem;
      border-radius:999px;
      padding:10px 20px;
      background:linear-gradient(90deg,#a855f7,#f59e0b);
      color:#0c0214;
      box-shadow:0 8px 22px rgba(168,85,247,0.65);
      position:relative;
      overflow:hidden;
      transform:translateY(0);
      transition:transform var(--transition), box-shadow var(--transition), opacity var(--transition);
      white-space:nowrap;
    }
    .btn-pill.secondary {
      background:rgba(249,250,251,0.08);
      color:var(--text-main);
      box-shadow:none;
      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14);
    }
    .btn-pill:active { transform:translateY(1px) scale(0.98); box-shadow:none; }

    /* Pulse + avatar + mode toggle */
    .pulse-row {
      display:flex;
      padding:10px 16px 6px;
      gap:12px;
    }
    .avatar-block {
      flex-shrink:0;
      display:flex;
      flex-direction:column;
      align-items:center;
      gap:6px;
    }
    .avatar-toggle-label {
      font-size:0.7rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      text-transform:uppercase;
      letter-spacing:0.14em;
    }
    .avatar-ring {
      width:84px; height:84px;
      border-radius:999px;
      border:3.5px solid rgba(168,85,247,0.7);
      box-shadow:0 0 18px rgba(168,85,247,0.7);
      display:flex;
      align-items:center;
      justify-content:center;
      position:relative;
      animation:spin 18s linear infinite;
      cursor:pointer;
    }
    .avatar-core {
      width:54px; height:54px;
      border-radius:999px;
      background:radial-gradient(circle,#a855f7 55%,#1a072a);
      box-shadow:0 0 30px #a855f7;
      transition:all var(--transition);
    }
    .avatar-core.pet {
      background:radial-gradient(circle,#a855f7 55%,#1f102f);
    }
    .avatar-core.prime {
      background:radial-gradient(circle,#f59e0b 25%,#7e22ce 75%);
      box-shadow:0 0 30px #f59e0b;
    }
    .avatar-core.ultra {
      background:conic-gradient(from 180deg,#a855f7,#f59e0b,#a855f7);
      box-shadow:0 0 42px #fbbf24;
    }
    .mode-chip {
      font-size:0.78rem;
      padding:3px 10px;
      border-radius:999px;
      background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
      border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.18);
      color:var(--text-dim);
    }

    @keyframes spin {
      100% { transform:rotate(360deg); }
    }

    .pulse-meta {
      flex:1;
      display:flex;
      flex-direction:column;
      gap:4px;
      justify-content:center;
    }
    .chip {
      display:inline-flex;
      align-items:center;
      gap:6px;
      padding:2px 10px;
      border-radius:999px;
      font-size:0.78rem;
      background:#20132f;
      color:var(--text-dim);
    }

    .tracker-card {
      padding:8px 16px 14px;
    }
    .tracker-label {
      display:flex;
      justify-content:space-between;
      font-size:0.8rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      margin-bottom:4px;
    }
    .progress-shell {
      width:100%;
      height:9px;
      border-radius:999px;
      background:rgba(255,255,255,0.07);
      overflow:hidden;
      margin-bottom:6px;
    }
    .progress-fill {
      height:100%;
      border-radius:999px;
      width:0;
      transition:width 0.3s ease;
    }
    .bar-listen {
      background:linear-gradient(90deg,#a855f7,#e9d5ff);
    }
    .bar-speak {
      background:linear-gradient(90deg,#f59e0b,#fef3c7);
    }

    .daily-buttons {
      display:flex;
      gap:10px;
      margin-top:8px;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
    }
    .mini-btn {
      flex:1;
      border:none;
      border-radius:999px;
      padding:8px 10px;
      font-size:0.78rem;
      font-weight:600;
      cursor:pointer;
      background:#a855f7;
      color:#fff;
      box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(168,85,247,0.6);
      transition:var(--transition);
    }
    .mini-btn.speak {
      background:var(--gold);
      color:#150506;
      box-shadow:0 6px 18px rgba(245,158,11,0.6);
    }
    .mini-btn:active { transform:scale(0.97); box-shadow:none; }

    /* Momentum / XP */
    .momentum-row {
      display:flex;
      padding:0 16px 12px;
      gap:10px;
    }
    .momentum-meter {
      width:20px;
      border-radius:999px;
      background:rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
      padding:3px;
      display:flex;
      flex-direction:column;
      justify-content:flex-end;
      align-items:stretch;
      position:relative;
      overflow:hidden;
    }
    .momentum-fill {
      width:100%;
      border-radius:999px;
      background:linear-gradient(180deg,#f59e0b,#a855f7);
      height:0;
      transition:height 0.3s ease;
      box-shadow:0 0 20px #a855f7aa;
    }
    .momentum-level-label {
      font-size:0.7rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      text-transform:uppercase;
    }
    .xp-card {
      flex:1;
      border-radius:var(--radius);
      background:rgba(0,0,0,0.75);
      padding:10px 12px;
    }
    .xp-row {
      display:flex;
      justify-content:space-between;
      font-size:0.76rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      margin-bottom:2px;
    }

    #aiLines {
      padding:4px 20px 8px;
      font-size:0.9rem;
      font-style:italic;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      min-height:46px;
    }

    /* Capabilities & FAQ pills */
    #capabilities, #faq {
      padding:4px 18px 10px;
    }
    .pill-block {
      display:grid;
      gap:8px;
    }
    .pill {
      display:inline-flex;
      align-items:center;
      gap:6px;
      padding:6px 10px;
      border-radius:999px;
      font-size:0.78rem;
      background:#a855f722;
      color:var(--text-main);
    }

    footer {
      padding:6px 18px 14px;
      font-size:0.72rem;
      color:var(--text-dim);
      text-align:right;
    }

    @media (max-width:480px) {
      .hero-card { margin-inline:12px; }
      header, .section { padding-inline:14px; }
      .pulse-row, .tracker-card, .momentum-row, #aiLines, #capabilities, #faq, footer { padding-inline:14px; }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="app-shell">
    <div class="app-inner">
      <header>
        <div>
          <div class="brand">NET PET AI · PHOENIX FUSION v2.0</div>
          <div class="tagline">Daily English coach. Tough love plus real care, zero grammar prison.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="streak-chip">
          <span class="streak-dot"></span>
          <span id="streakNum">0</span> days
        </div>
      </header>

      <section class="hero-card">
        <div class="section-label">Accountability engine</div>
        <div class="hero-title">
          Build your <span class="hero-accent">English lifestyle</span><br>45 · 15 · every day.
        </div>
        <div class="hero-sub">
          Listen like a fan, speak like a tiger. NET PET tracks it. You just show up.
        </div>
        <div class="hero-cta-row">
          <button class="btn-pill" id="ctaLog">Log today</button>
          <button class="btn-pill secondary" id="ctaMore">What can this coach do?</button>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="pulse-row">
        <div class="avatar-block">
          <div class="avatar-toggle-label">Mode</div>
          <div class="avatar-ring" id="avatarRing">
            <div class="avatar-core pet" id="avatarCore"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="mode-chip" id="modeChip">NET PET · Coach</div>
        </div>
        <div class="pulse-meta">
          <div class="chip" id="chipMood">Mood: Neutral</div>
          <div class="chip" id="chipGoal" style="background:#f59e0b24;color:#f59e0b;">Goal: Not complete</div>
          <div id="pulseStatus" style="font-size:0.92rem;margin-top:4px;">
            Let’s check: tiger or mouse today?
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="tracker-card">
        <div class="tracker-label">
          <span>Listening (Fun input)</span>
          <span id="lblListen">0 / 45m</span>
        </div>
        <div class="progress-shell">
          <div class="progress-fill bar-listen" id="barListen"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="tracker-label">
          <span>Speaking (Shadow / volume)</span>
          <span id="lblSpeak">0 / 15m</span>
        </div>
        <div class="progress-shell">
          <div class="progress-fill bar-speak" id="barSpeak"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="daily-buttons">
          <button class="mini-btn" id="btnListen">+15m Listening</button>
          <button class="mini-btn speak" id="btnSpeak">+5m Speaking</button>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="momentum-row">
        <div class="momentum-meter">
          <div class="momentum-fill" id="momentumFill"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="xp-card">
          <div class="momentum-level-label" id="momentumLabel">Level 1 · Struggling Mouse</div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Consistency XP</span><span id="xpConsistency">0</span></div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Growth XP</span><span id="xpGrowth">0</span></div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Courage XP</span><span id="xpCourage">0</span></div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Tiger XP</span><span id="xpTiger">0</span></div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section id="aiLines"></section>
      <section id="capabilities"></section>
      <section id="faq"></section>

      <footer>GOSPEL 444 · PHOENIX ENGINE · CHATTTY PRIME × NET PET</footer>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // ===== EVENT BUS (for future agents / sockets / PWA hooks) =====
    const Bus = {
      subs:{},
      on(ev, fn){ (this.subs[ev] = this.subs[ev] || []).push(fn); },
      emit(ev, payload){ (this.subs[ev] || []).forEach(fn => fn(payload)); }
    };

    // ===== STATE ENGINE =====
    const BASE = {
      streak: 0,
      listen: 0,
      speak: 0,
      last: today(),
      mood: "NEUTRAL", // NEUTRAL | HAPPY | TOUGH
      goal: false,
      xpConsistency: 0,
      xpGrowth: 0,
      xpCourage: 0,
      xpTiger: 0,
      mode: "PET" // PET | PRIME | ULTRA
    };

    function today() {
      return new Date().toLocaleDateString();
    }

    let state = loadState();
    rolloverDay(); // in case they open after midnight

    function loadState() {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem("netPet_v2_state");
        if (!raw) return { ...BASE };
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        return { ...BASE, ...parsed };
      } catch {
        return { ...BASE };
      }
    }

    function saveState() {
      localStorage.setItem("netPet_v2_state", JSON.stringify(state));
      // simple hash snapshot for drift detection / debug
      localStorage.setItem("netPet_v2_hash", btoa(JSON.stringify({
        streak: state.streak,
        listen: state.listen,
        speak: state.speak,
        xp: totalXP()
      })));
    }

    function rolloverDay() {
      const todayStr = today();
      if (state.last !== todayStr) {
        // streak logic
        if (state.goal) {
          state.streak += 1;
          state.mood = "HAPPY";
          state.xpConsistency += 10;
        } else {
          state.streak = 0;
          state.mood = "TOUGH";
          state.xpCourage += 5; // you at least opened the app
        }
        state.listen = 0;
        state.speak = 0;
        state.goal = false;
        state.last = todayStr;
        saveState();
      }
    }

    function totalXP() {
      return state.xpConsistency + state.xpGrowth + state.xpCourage + state.xpTiger;
    }

    // ===== VOICE MATRICES =====
    const LINES = {
      PET: {
        GREETING: [
          "Every day is a new chance to build your English lifestyle.",
          "You don’t need perfection. You need today’s 45·15.",
          "Listen like a fan, speak like a tiger.",
          "We’re not studying English. We’re living with it."
        ],
        HAPPY: [
          "Goal done. This is what real learning looks like.",
          "You’re not a robot. You’re a human speaking English.",
          "This is momentum. Don’t break it.",
          "Nice. Tomorrow, same time, same energy."
        ],
        TOUGH: [
          "You can’t learn to swim by looking at the water.",
          "No speaking yet? Your mouth is the muscle. Use it.",
          "One small set now is better than a perfect plan later.",
          "You don’t need motivation. You need 5 nervous minutes."
        ]
      },
      PRIME: {
        GREETING: [
          "Status: online. Your behavior is the only bottleneck.",
          "System check: zero grammar drills required. Only action.",
          "Audit result: today has no log yet. That is a bug.",
          "You are the only person who can fix this sprint."
        ],
        HAPPY: [
          "Daily KPIs met. Confidence curve trending upward.",
          "Streak integrity preserved. Ship again tomorrow.",
          "Data suggests: this version of you speaks more.",
          "You are compounding tiny wins. That’s engineering."
        ],
        TOUGH: [
          "No log. No progress. This is not a training run.",
          "You don’t fix speaking with theory. Only reps.",
          "Your future self is watching this decision.",
          "If you want new output, update your behavior."
        ]
      },
      ULTRA: {
        FUSION: [
          "Seven days of discipline. You’re not learning English, you’re becoming someone new.",
          "This is Phoenix Mode: consistent, dangerous, and fluent.",
          "You broke the cage. Now we build the new world.",
          "From grammar prison to Phoenix. Keep burning forward."
        ]
      }
    };

    function pickLine(type) {
      const mode = state.mode === "ULTRA"
        ? "ULTRA"
        : (state.mode === "PRIME" ? "PRIME" : "PET");

      if (mode === "ULTRA") {
        const pool = LINES.ULTRA.FUSION;
        return pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
      }

      const moodKey = type || (state.mood === "HAPPY" ? "HAPPY" : state.mood === "TOUGH" ? "TOUGH" : "GREETING");
      const pool = LINES[mode][moodKey] || LINES[mode].GREETING;
      return pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * pool.length)];
    }

    // ===== XP / LEVEL LOGIC =====
    function levelInfo() {
      const xp = totalXP();
      if (xp >= 400) return { level: 5, label: "Phoenix Mode", momentum: 100 };
      if (xp >= 250) return { level: 4, label: "Tiger Mode", momentum: 80 };
      if (xp >= 120) return { level: 3, label: "Consistent", momentum: 60 };
      if (xp >= 50)  return { level: 2, label: "Waking Up", momentum: 40 };
      return { level: 1, label: "Struggling Mouse", momentum: 20 };
    }

    function maybeUnlockUltra() {
      const info = levelInfo();
      if (info.level >= 5 && state.mode === "PET") {
        state.mode = "ULTRA";
      }
    }

    // ===== EVALUATION =====
    function evaluateProgress(logType) {
      // Basic goal condition
      const beforeGoal = state.goal;
      if (state.listen >= 45 && state.speak >= 15) {
        state.goal = true;
        state.mood = "HAPPY";
        if (!beforeGoal) {
          state.xpGrowth += 10;
          if (logType === "speak") state.xpTiger += 5;
        }
      } else if (state.listen > 0 && state.speak === 0) {
        state.mood = "TOUGH";
      } else {
        state.mood = "NEUTRAL";
      }

      // Courage XP: first speaking log of the day
      if (logType === "speak" && state.speak === 5) {
        state.xpCourage += 3;
      }

      maybeUnlockUltra();
    }

    // ===== RENDER =====
    const els = {
      streakNum: document.getElementById("streakNum"),
      lblListen: document.getElementById("lblListen"),
      lblSpeak: document.getElementById("lblSpeak"),
      barListen: document.getElementById("barListen"),
      barSpeak: document.getElementById("barSpeak"),
      chipMood: document.getElementById("chipMood"),
      chipGoal: document.getElementById("chipGoal"),
      pulseStatus: document.getElementById("pulseStatus"),
      avatarCore: document.getElementById("avatarCore"),
      modeChip: document.getElementById("modeChip"),
      aiLines: document.getElementById("aiLines"),
      momentumFill: document.getElementById("momentumFill"),
      momentumLabel: document.getElementById("momentumLabel"),
      xpConsistency: document.getElementById("xpConsistency"),
      xpGrowth: document.getElementById("xpGrowth"),
      xpCourage: document.getElementById("xpCourage"),
      xpTiger: document.getElementById("xpTiger")
    };

    function render() {
      els.streakNum.textContent = state.streak;
      els.lblListen.textContent = `${state.listen} / 45m`;
      els.lblSpeak.textContent = `${state.speak} / 15m`;

      els.barListen.style.width = Math.min(100, (state.listen / 45) * 100) + "%";
      els.barSpeak.style.width = Math.min(100, (state.speak / 15) * 100) + "%";

      const moodTxt = state.mood === "HAPPY" ? "Happy"
                        : state.mood === "TOUGH" ? "Tough Love"
                        : "Neutral";
      els.chipMood.textContent = `Mood: ${moodTxt}`;
      els.chipGoal.textContent = "Goal: " + (state.goal ? "Complete" : "Not complete");

      // mode chip + avatar
      els.avatarCore.className = "avatar-core " +
        (state.mode === "ULTRA" ? "ultra" : state.mode === "PRIME" ? "prime" : "pet");
      els.modeChip.textContent =
        state.mode === "ULTRA" ? "Phoenix Fusion · Coach+Prime" :
        state.mode === "PRIME" ? "Chatty Prime · Auditor" :
        "NET PET · Coach";

      // pulse text
      if (state.mood === "TOUGH") {
        els.pulseStatus.textContent = "Slump detected. We fix it with one small rep, not guilt.";
      } else if (state.mood === "HAPPY") {
        els.pulseStatus.textContent = "Goal hit. This is how confidence is built, not wished for.";
      } else {
        els.pulseStatus.textContent = "Tiny daily actions. Big long-term upgrades.";
      }

      // XP + level
      const info = levelInfo();
      els.momentumFill.style.height = info.momentum + "%";
      els.momentumLabel.textContent = `Level ${info.level} · ${info.label}`;
      els.xpConsistency.textContent = state.xpConsistency;
      els.xpGrowth.textContent = state.xpGrowth;
      els.xpCourage.textContent = state.xpCourage;
      els.xpTiger.textContent = state.xpTiger;

      els.aiLines.textContent = pickLine();
    }

    // ===== CAPABILITIES & FAQ STATIC RENDER =====
    function renderStaticSections() {
      const cap = document.getElementById("capabilities");
      cap.innerHTML = `
        <div class="section-label">What can this coach do?</div>
        <div class="pill-block">
          <div class="pill">🐯 Push your comfort zone when you hide behind silence.</div>
          <div class="pill">📊 Track your 45·15 habit with streaks, XP, and momentum.</div>
          <div class="pill">🧠 Switch voices: soft human coach or strict auditor.</div>
          <div class="pill">🔥 Unlock Phoenix Fusion when you prove you're consistent.</div>
        </div>
      `;
      const faq = document.getElementById("faq");
      faq.innerHTML = `
        <div class="section-label">FAQ</div>
        <div class="pill">❓ What if I miss a day? — Your streak resets. No drama. Just start again today.</div>
        <div class="pill">❓ Do I need grammar books? — No. Listen, mimic, and speak. Grammar joins later.</div>
        <div class="pill">❓ Is this a teacher? — This is your daily English coach. Human classes are extra firepower.</div>
      `;
    }

    // ===== EVENT LISTENERS =====
    document.getElementById("btnListen").addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.listen = Math.min(45, state.listen + 15);
      evaluateProgress("listen");
      saveState();
      render();
      Bus.emit("log", { type:"listen", state:{...state} });
    });

    document.getElementById("btnSpeak").addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.speak = Math.min(15, state.speak + 5);
      evaluateProgress("speak");
      saveState();
      render();
      Bus.emit("log", { type:"speak", state:{...state} });
    });

    document.getElementById("ctaLog").addEventListener("click", () => {
      const target = document.querySelector(".tracker-card");
      if (!target) return;
      window.scrollTo({ top: target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - 50, behavior: "smooth" });
    });

    document.getElementById("ctaMore").addEventListener("click", () => {
      const target = document.getElementById("capabilities");
      window.scrollTo({ top: target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - 40, behavior: "smooth" });
    });

    document.getElementById("avatarRing").addEventListener("click", () => {
      // cycle modes: PET -> PRIME -> ULTRA (if unlocked) -> PET ...
      const info = levelInfo();
      const ultraUnlocked = info.level >= 5;
      if (state.mode === "PET") {
        state.mode = "PRIME";
      } else if (state.mode === "PRIME") {
        state.mode = ultraUnlocked ? "ULTRA" : "PET";
      } else {
        state.mode = "PET";
      }
      saveState();
      render();
    });

    // Example Bus listener (extendable)
    Bus.on("log", payload => {
      // future: sync to backend, show confetti, etc.
      // console.log("NET PET log event:", payload);
    });

    // ===== INIT =====
    renderStaticSections();
    saveState();
    render();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

---

## 2️⃣ Split version — `index.html`, `styles.css`, `app.js`

Same app, but separated.

### `index.html`

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>

      <section class="momentum-row">
        <div class="momentum-meter">

        </div>
        <div class="xp-card">
          <div class="momentum-level-label" id="momentumLabel">Level 1 · Struggling Mouse</div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Consistency XP</span><span id="xpConsistency">0</span></div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Growth XP</span><span id="xpGrowth">0</span></div>
          <div class="xp-row"><span>Courage XP</span><span id="xpCourage">0</span></div>

</body>

```

### `styles.css`

Use the **same CSS** as in the `<style>` block of the standalone file (section 1).
Just copy that `<style> ... </style>` content into `styles.css` (without the `<style>` tags).

### `app.js`

Use the **same JS** as in the `<script>` block of the standalone file (section 1).
Just copy inside `app.js` (without the `<script>` tags).

---

## 3️⃣ PWA bits — `manifest.json` + `service-worker.js`

### `manifest.json`

```json
{
  "name": "NET PET AI Coach — Phoenix Fusion",
  "short_name": "NET PET",
  "start_url": "./index.html",
  "display": "standalone",
  "background_color": "#0f0f1a",
  "theme_color": "#0f0f1a",
  "icons": [
    {
      "src": "icon-192.png",
      "sizes": "192x192",
      "type": "image/png"
    },
    {
      "src": "icon-512.png",
      "sizes": "512x512",
      "type": "image/png"
    }
  ]
}
```

(You can generate simple purple/gold icons later.)

### `service-worker.js`

```js
const CACHE_NAME = "net-pet-v2-cache-v1";
const ASSETS = [
  "./",
  "./index.html",
  "./styles.css",
  "./app.js",
  "./manifest.json"
];

self.addEventListener("install", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => cache.addAll(ASSETS))
  );
});

self.addEventListener("activate", (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then((keys) =>
      Promise.all(
        keys
          .filter((key) => key !== CACHE_NAME)
          .map((key) => caches.delete(key))
      )
    )
  );
});

self.addEventListener("fetch", (event) => {
  event.respondWith(
    caches.match(event.request).then((resp) => {
      return resp || fetch(event.request);
    })
  );
});
```

Then, in `app.js` (or a tiny extra script), register the worker:

```js
if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
  window.addEventListener("load", () => {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register("./service-worker.js").catch(() => {});
  });
}
```

---

## 4️⃣ Capacitor scaffold (Android / iOS ready)

Minimal structure:

```text
net-pet-app/
   package.json
   capacitor.config.ts (or .json)
   index.html
   styles.css
   app.js
   manifest.json
   service-worker.js
   (android/ and ios/ will be generated)
```

### `package.json`

```json
{
  "name": "net-pet-ai-coach",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "private": true,
  "scripts": {
    "build": "echo \"Static app — no build step\"",
    "serve": "npx serve .",
    "capacitor:sync": "npx cap sync",
    "capacitor:android": "npx cap open android",
    "capacitor:ios": "npx cap open ios"
  },
  "dependencies": {
    "@capacitor/core": "^6.0.0"
  },
  "devDependencies": {
    "@capacitor/cli": "^6.0.0"
  }
}
```

### `capacitor.config.ts` (or `.json`)

TypeScript version:

```ts
import { CapacitorConfig } from '@capacitor/cli';

const config: CapacitorConfig = {
  appId: 'com.net.pet',
  appName: 'NET PET AI Coach',
  webDir: '.',
  bundledWebRuntime: false
};

export default config;
```

Or `capacitor.config.json`:

```json
{
  "appId": "com.net.pet",
  "appName": "NET PET AI Coach",
  "webDir": ".",
  "bundledWebRuntime": false
}
```

### Basic flow

From the `net-pet-app` folder:

```bash
npm install
npx cap init "NET PET AI Coach" "com.net.pet"  # if not using config file yet
npx cap sync
npx cap open android   # or ios
```

Capacitor will wrap your existing `index.html + styles.css + app.js + manifest + sw` as a mobile app.
