Save this as e.g. `net-pet-ai-v1.html` and open in a mobile browser (or narrow desktop window) 👇

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <title>NET PET AI Coach — V1</title>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
  <style>
    /* =========================================================================
       GOSPEL 444 AESTHETICS — PHOENIX × COMET HYBRID
       Colors: Neon Purple, Gold, Deep Dark. NO BLUE.
       ========================================================================= */
    :root {
      --neon-purple: #a855f7;
      --gold: #f59e0b;
      --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
      --glass-bg: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.82);
      --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
      --text-primary: #f9fafb;
      --text-dim: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.7);
      --danger: #ef4444;
      --success: #22c55e;
      --radius-card: 24px;
      --blur-glass: 16px;
      --shadow-soft: 0 20px 60px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8);
      --transition-fast: 0.18s ease-out;
    }

    * {
      box-sizing: border-box;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
    }

    html, body {
      height: 100%;
    }

    body {
      font-family: "Inter", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
      background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 15% 20%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.16) 0%, transparent 55%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 85% 80%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.16) 0%, transparent 55%),
        var(--deep-dark);
      color: var(--text-primary);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: center;
      align-items: stretch;
    }

    /* Star / Comet backdrop */
    body::before {
      content: "";
      position: fixed;
      inset: 0;
      background-image:
        radial-gradient(circle at 20% 10%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15) 0, transparent 40%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 80% 90%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08) 0, transparent 45%);
      opacity: 0.18;
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 0;
    }

    body::after {
      content: "";
      position: fixed;
      top: -40%;
      left: -20%;
      width: 140%;
      height: 140%;
      background: conic-gradient(
        from 200deg,
        rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.08),
        rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.12),
        rgba(0, 0, 0, 0),
        rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.08)
      );
      mix-blend-mode: screen;
      opacity: 0.7;
      filter: blur(32px);
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 0;
    }

    /* App shell */
    .app-shell {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 1;
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 480px;
      height: 100vh;
      padding: 18px 14px 20px;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }

    .app-inner {
      flex: 1;
      background: var(--glass-bg);
      border-radius: 32px;
      backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-glass));
      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-glass));
      border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
      box-shadow: var(--shadow-soft);
      overflow: hidden;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
    }

    header.app-header {
      padding: 14px 18px 10px;
      border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.06);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 10px;
    }

    .brand-block {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 2px;
    }

    .brand-line {
      font-family: "JetBrains Mono", monospace;
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      letter-spacing: 0.12em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      color: var(--gold);
    }

    .brand-tagline {
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .streak-chip {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
      padding: 4px 10px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4), rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7));
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--text-primary);
    }

    .streak-dot {
      width: 9px;
      height: 9px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: var(--gold);
      box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.9);
    }

    main.app-main {
      flex: 1;
      overflow-y: auto;
      padding: 10px 14px 16px;
      scroll-behavior: smooth;
    }

    main.app-main::-webkit-scrollbar {
      width: 0;
    }

    /* Sections */
    section {
      margin-bottom: 16px;
    }

    .section-label {
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.12em;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      margin-bottom: 6px;
    }

    .hero-card {
      border-radius: var(--radius-card);
      padding: 14px 16px 16px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 10% 0%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2), transparent 60%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 80% 120%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.35), transparent 60%),
        rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.78);
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.07);
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .hero-title {
      font-size: 1.35rem;
      font-weight: 800;
      line-height: 1.25;
      margin-bottom: 6px;
    }

    .hero-accent {
      color: var(--gold);
    }

    .hero-subtitle {
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      max-width: 260px;
    }

    .hero-cta-row {
      margin-top: 12px;
      display: flex;
      gap: 8px;
    }

    .btn-pill {
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 8px 14px;
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      border: none;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition:
        transform var(--transition-fast),
        box-shadow var(--transition-fast),
        background var(--transition-fast),
        color var(--transition-fast);
      font-family: "Inter", sans-serif;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }

    .btn-primary {
      background: linear-gradient(120deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--gold));
      color: #0a0412;
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
    }

    .btn-secondary {
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);
      color: var(--text-primary);
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.14);
    }

    .btn-pill:active {
      transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.98);
      box-shadow: none;
    }

    /* Hero comet arc */
    .hero-comet {
      position: absolute;
      top: 10%;
      right: -20%;
      width: 220px;
      height: 220px;
      background: conic-gradient(
        from 220deg,
        rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.9),
        rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.0),
        rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.8),
        rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)
      );
      opacity: 0.22;
      filter: blur(22px);
      transform-origin: left center;
      animation: comet-glide 18s linear infinite;
      pointer-events: none;
    }

    @keyframes comet-glide {
      0% {
        transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0) rotate(6deg);
        opacity: 0.24;
      }
      40% {
        transform: translate3d(-14px, 12px, 0) rotate(18deg);
        opacity: 0.4;
      }
      80% {
        transform: translate3d(-28px, -4px, 0) rotate(0deg);
        opacity: 0.18;
      }
      100% {
        transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0) rotate(6deg);
        opacity: 0.24;
      }
    }

    /* Avatar / Pulse section */
    .pulse-card {
      border-radius: var(--radius-card);
      padding: 12px 14px 14px;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.74);
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.06);
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: auto 1fr;
      gap: 12px;
      align-items: center;
    }

    .avatar-stack {
      position: relative;
      width: 84px;
      height: 84px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
    }

    .avatar-ring {
      position: absolute;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 2px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
      animation: spin-ring 18s linear infinite;
    }

    .avatar-ring.inner {
      width: 70px;
      height: 70px;
      border: 1px dashed rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.6);
      animation: spin-ring 18s linear infinite reverse;
    }

    @keyframes spin-ring {
      100% {
        transform: rotate(360deg);
      }
    }

    .avatar-core {
      width: 54px;
      height: 54px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--neon-purple), #3b0764);
      box-shadow: 0 0 26px rgba(168, 85, 247, 1);
      transition: all 0.28s ease-out;
    }

    .avatar-core.happy {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--gold), #b45309);
      box-shadow: 0 0 32px rgba(245, 158, 11, 1);
      transform: scale(1.06);
    }

    .avatar-core.slump {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, #7f1d1d, #450a0a);
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.9);
      opacity: 0.85;
    }

    .pulse-meta {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 4px;
    }

    .pulse-title {
      font-size: 0.92rem;
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    .pulse-sub {
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .pulse-chip-row {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: 6px;
      margin-top: 4px;
    }

    .chip {
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 2px 8px;
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.14);
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .chip-heat {
      border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5);
      color: var(--gold);
    }

    /* Dialogue */
    .dialogue-card {
      margin-top: 10px;
      border-radius: var(--radius-card);
      padding: 10px 12px;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.68);
      border-left: 3px solid var(--neon-purple);
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      line-height: 1.5;
      font-style: italic;
      color: var(--text-primary);
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .dialogue-card::before {
      content: "NET PET says";
      position: absolute;
      top: 6px;
      right: 10px;
      font-size: 0.65rem;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.14em;
      color: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.28);
      font-style: normal;
    }

    /* Tracker */
    .tracker-card {
      border-radius: var(--radius-card);
      padding: 12px 14px 14px;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.78);
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.06);
    }

    .tracker-row {
      margin-bottom: 10px;
    }

    .tracker-label {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      margin-bottom: 4px;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    }

    .progress-shell {
      width: 100%;
      height: 10px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.08);
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .progress-fill {
      height: 100%;
      width: 0%;
      border-radius: 999px;
      transition: width 0.4s ease-out;
    }

    .progress-fill.listen {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), #e9d5ff);
    }

    .progress-fill.speak {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--gold), #facc15);
    }

    .tracker-buttons {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
      gap: 8px;
      margin-top: 8px;
    }

    .btn-solid {
      width: 100%;
      border-radius: 14px;
      padding: 10px 8px;
      border: none;
      font-size: 0.82rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition:
        transform var(--transition-fast),
        box-shadow var(--transition-fast),
        background var(--transition-fast),
        color var(--transition-fast);
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      gap: 6px;
      font-family: "Inter", sans-serif;
    }

    .btn-listen {
      background: var(--neon-purple);
      color: #0b0414;
      box-shadow: 0 8px 22px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.46);
    }

    .btn-speak {
      background: var(--gold);
      color: #120706;
      box-shadow: 0 8px 22px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.46);
    }

    .btn-solid:active {
      transform: translateY(1px) scale(0.985);
      box-shadow: none;
    }

    .tracker-footnote {
      margin-top: 6px;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    /* Capabilities (COMET-style "AI that…") */
    .capabilities-card {
      border-radius: var(--radius-card);
      padding: 12px 12px 10px;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.74);
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.06);
    }

    .capabilities-grid {
      display: grid;
      gap: 8px;
    }

    .capability-pill {
      border-radius: 18px;
      padding: 8px 10px;
      background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.92);
      border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.42);
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .cap-title {
      font-size: 0.84rem;
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    .cap-badge {
      font-size: 0.68rem;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.12em;
      color: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.5);
      margin-bottom: 2px;
    }

    .cap-text {
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      margin-top: 2px;
    }

    .capability-pill::after {
      content: "";
      position: absolute;
      right: -30px;
      top: -30px;
      width: 80px;
      height: 80px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25), transparent 65%);
      opacity: 0.6;
    }

    /* FAQ */
    .faq-card {
      border-radius: var(--radius-card);
      padding: 10px 12px;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.78);
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.06);
    }

    .faq-item {
      border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.08);
    }

    .faq-item:last-child {
      border-bottom: none;
    }

    .faq-header {
      padding: 8px 2px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 8px;
      cursor: pointer;
      font-size: 0.84rem;
    }

    .faq-question {
      flex: 1;
    }

    .faq-toggle {
      width: 16px;
      height: 16px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.4);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      color: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.6);
    }

    .faq-body {
      max-height: 0;
      overflow: hidden;
      transition: max-height 0.25s ease-out;
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      padding: 0 2px;
    }

    .faq-body-inner {
      padding-bottom: 8px;
    }

    .faq-item.open .faq-body {
      max-height: 200px;
    }

    .faq-item.open .faq-toggle {
      border-color: var(--gold);
      color: var(--gold);
    }

    /* Footer */
    footer.app-footer {
      padding: 6px 14px 8px;
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
    }

    .footer-mark {
      font-family: "JetBrains Mono", monospace;
      letter-spacing: 0.15em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      color: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.4);
      font-size: 0.62rem;
    }

    .foot-pill {
      padding: 4px 8px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.18);
    }

    @media (max-width: 380px) {
      .hero-title {
        font-size: 1.18rem;
      }
      .hero-subtitle {
        font-size: 0.82rem;
      }
      .pulse-card {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="app-shell">
    <div class="app-inner">
      <header class="app-header">
        <div class="brand-block">
          <div class="brand-line">NET PET AI · V1</div>
          <div class="brand-tagline">Your daily English coach, not a textbook.</div>
        </div>
        <div class="streak-chip">
          <span class="streak-dot"></span>
          <span><span id="streak-count">0</span> day streak</span>
        </div>
      </header>

      <main class="app-main" id="scroll-root">
        <!-- HERO -->
        <section id="section-hero">
          <div class="hero-card">
            <div class="hero-comet"></div>
            <div class="section-label">The coach that works for you</div>
            <h1 class="hero-title">
              Build your <span class="hero-accent">English lifestyle</span>
              <br />45 · 15 · Every day.
            </h1>
            <p class="hero-subtitle">
              NET PET tracks your listening, pushes your speaking, and refuses to let you stay a mouse.
            </p>
            <div class="hero-cta-row">
              <button class="btn-pill btn-primary" id="cta-log-today">
                Log today’s study
              </button>
              <button class="btn-pill btn-secondary" id="cta-what-can">
                What can NET PET do?
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- PULSE + DIALOGUE -->
        <section id="section-pulse">
          <div class="section-label">Today’s energy</div>
          <div class="pulse-card">
            <div class="avatar-stack">
              <div class="avatar-ring"></div>
              <div class="avatar-ring inner"></div>
              <div class="avatar-core" id="pet-avatar"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="pulse-meta">
              <div class="pulse-title">Your English Energy</div>
              <div class="pulse-sub" id="pulse-status">
                Let’s see if today looks like a tiger or a mouse.
              </div>
              <div class="pulse-chip-row">
                <div class="chip chip-heat" id="chip-mood">Mood: Neutral</div>
                <div class="chip" id="chip-goal">Goal: Not complete</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="dialogue-card">
            <div id="ai-line">
              "Initializing neural link. I’m here to keep you honest, not comfortable."
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- TRACKER -->
        <section id="section-tracker">
          <div class="section-label">45 / 15 tracker</div>
          <div class="tracker-card">
            <div class="tracker-row">
              <div class="tracker-label">
                <span>Listening (Fun Input)</span>
                <span id="listen-label">0 / 45m</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-shell">
                <div class="progress-fill listen" id="bar-listen"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tracker-row">
              <div class="tracker-label">
                <span>Speaking / Mimic</span>
                <span id="speak-label">0 / 15m</span>
              </div>
              <div class="progress-shell">
                <div class="progress-fill speak" id="bar-speak"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="tracker-buttons">
              <button class="btn-solid btn-listen" id="btn-log-listen">+15m Listening</button>
              <button class="btn-solid btn-speak" id="btn-log-speak">+5m Speaking</button>
            </div>
            <div class="tracker-footnote">
              Rule #1: 60% understanding minimum. Rule #2: No pausing. Rule #3: Make it fun.
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- CAPABILITIES — COMET STYLE AI THAT… -->
        <section id="section-capabilities">
          <div class="section-label">What NET PET can do</div>
          <div class="capabilities-card">
            <div class="capabilities-grid">
              <div class="capability-pill">
                <div class="cap-badge">AI that coaches</div>
                <div class="cap-title">Tough love when you’re hiding</div>
                <p class="cap-text">
                  If you only listen and never speak, NET PET flips into Coach Mode and pushes you out
                  of your comfort zone. No more “I’ll speak when I’m perfect”.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div class="capability-pill">
                <div class="cap-badge">AI that tracks</div>
                <div class="cap-title">Your 45·15 streak, day by day</div>
                <p class="cap-text">
                  It watches your listening and speaking time, day rollovers, and streaks – then
                  reacts when you break the habit or level up.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div class="capability-pill">
                <div class="cap-badge">AI that builds</div>
                <div class="cap-title">A lifestyle, not a cram session</div>
                <p class="cap-text">
                  Instead of “homework”, NET PET designs small, realistic daily wins so English becomes
                  part of your lifestyle, not a chore.
                </p>
              </div>
              <div class="capability-pill">
                <div class="cap-badge">AI that fixes</div>
                <div class="cap-title">Robot voice & mouse energy</div>
                <p class="cap-text">
                  It keeps reminding you to increase volume, mimic emotion, and laugh at mistakes –
                  so you stop sounding like a shy exam robot.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- FAQ -->
        <section id="section-faq">
          <div class="section-label">FAQ</div>
          <div class="faq-card">
            <div class="faq-item">
              <div class="faq-header">
                <div class="faq-question">What does NET PET actually track?</div>
                <div class="faq-toggle">+</div>
              </div>
              <div class="faq-body">
                <div class="faq-body-inner">
                  Your daily listening minutes, speaking minutes, and whether you’ve hit the 45/15 goal
                  for the day. It also tracks your streak and mood state (neutral, tough love, proud).
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="faq-item">
              <div class="faq-header">
                <div class="faq-question">What happens if I miss a day?</div>
                <div class="faq-toggle">+</div>
              </div>
              <div class="faq-body">
                <div class="faq-body-inner">
                  If you miss your goal, the next day NET PET resets your streak and switches into
                  Tough Love mode. It doesn’t punish you – it just refuses to lie about your progress.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="faq-item">
              <div class="faq-header">
                <div class="faq-question">Do I have to log exact minutes?</div>
                <div class="faq-toggle">+</div>
              </div>
              <div class="faq-body">
                <div class="faq-body-inner">
                  No. The buttons are designed for quick logging. Each tap is a chunk of time:
                  15 minutes for listening, 5 minutes for speaking. You can tap multiple times
                  to match your real study.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="faq-item">
              <div class="faq-header">
                <div class="faq-question">Is this a replacement for a teacher?</div>
                <div class="faq-toggle">+</div>
              </div>
              <div class="faq-body">
                <div class="faq-body-inner">
                  No. NET PET is the daily accountability partner. The real transformation comes from
                  the combination of human teacher, NET lessons, and this AI coach watching your habits.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>

      <footer class="app-footer">
        <span class="footer-mark">GOSPEL·444</span>
        <span class="foot-pill">Phoenix Engine · Buddy System Ready</span>
      </footer>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // ========================================================================
    // PHOENIX EVENT BUS (COMET-STYLE CORE)
    // ========================================================================
    const EventBus = {
      _subscribers: {},
      on(event, fn) {
        if (!this._subscribers[event]) this._subscribers[event] = [];
        this._subscribers[event].push(fn);
      },
      emit(event, payload) {
        (this._subscribers[event] || []).forEach(fn => fn(payload));
      }
    };

    // ========================================================================
    // STATE & CANONICAL CONSTANTS
    // ========================================================================
    const DEFAULT_STATE = {
      streak: 0,
      listeningMin: 0,
      speakingMin: 0,
      lastLogin: new Date().toDateString(),
      mood: "NEUTRAL", // NEUTRAL, TOUGH_LOVE, HAPPY, PROUD
      dailyGoalMet: false
    };

    let state;
    try {
      const fromStorage = localStorage.getItem("netPetV1State");
      state = fromStorage ? { ...DEFAULT_STATE, ...JSON.parse(fromStorage) } : { ...DEFAULT_STATE };
    } catch {
      state = { ...DEFAULT_STATE };
    }

    const GOAL_LISTEN = 45;
    const GOAL_SPEAK = 15;

    // Voice matrix grounded in your canonical tones
    const VOICE_MATRIX = {
      GREETING: [
        "Let’s get to work. Consistency beats intensity.",
        "Welcome back. Ready to break the grammar prison again?",
        "Remember: you learned Vietnamese by listening first. We do the same here.",
        "Today is another chance to be a tiger, not a mouse."
      ],
      TOUGH_LOVE: [
        "You haven’t practiced speaking yet. You can’t learn to swim by reading the water.",
        "Grammar won’t save you in a real meeting. Confidence will. Open your mouth.",
        "You built a whole wall around your mistakes. Let’s kick one brick out today.",
        "Do you want fluency or just the feeling of ‘studying’? The difference is action."
      ],
      CELEBRATION: [
        "Boom. You hit 45 and 15. That’s real English work, not homework.",
        "That’s the energy I want. Small wins, every day. This is how you become fluent.",
        "You’re not ‘learning English’ anymore – you’re living with English.",
        "Outstanding. Tomorrow, we just repeat this. Simple. Heavy. Effective."
      ],
      NUDGE: [
        "Pick one show you actually enjoy and listen. No pausing. Just let it wash over you.",
        "Five more minutes of shadowing could change how you sound next month.",
        "60% understanding is enough. Your brain will fill the rest if you keep going.",
        "If it’s fun, you’ll keep coming back. Let’s make the next 10 minutes fun."
      ]
    };

    // DOM Refs
    const streakCountEl = document.getElementById("streak-count");
    const avatarEl = document.getElementById("pet-avatar");
    const chipMoodEl = document.getElementById("chip-mood");
    const chipGoalEl = document.getElementById("chip-goal");
    const pulseStatusEl = document.getElementById("pulse-status");

    const listenLabelEl = document.getElementById("listen-label");
    const speakLabelEl = document.getElementById("speak-label");
    const listenBarEl = document.getElementById("bar-listen");
    const speakBarEl = document.getElementById("bar-speak");

    const aiLineEl = document.getElementById("ai-line");

    const btnLogListen = document.getElementById("btn-log-listen");
    const btnLogSpeak = document.getElementById("btn-log-speak");
    const ctaLogToday = document.getElementById("cta-log-today");
    const ctaWhatCan = document.getElementById("cta-what-can");
    const mainRoot = document.getElementById("scroll-root");

    const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll(".faq-item");

    // ========================================================================
    // HELPERS
    // ========================================================================
    function saveState() {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("netPetV1State", JSON.stringify(state));
      } catch {
        // ignore storage errors
      }
      EventBus.emit("state:updated", { ...state });
    }

    function pickRandom(arr) {
      return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
    }

    function setLine(category) {
      const pool = VOICE_MATRIX[category];
      if (!pool || pool.length === 0) return;
      const line = pickRandom(pool);
      aiLineEl.textContent = `"${line}"`;
    }

    function todayString() {
      return new Date().toDateString();
    }

    // Check day rollover / slump detection
    function checkDayRollover() {
      const today = todayString();
      if (state.lastLogin !== today) {
        // New day
        if (state.dailyGoalMet) {
          state.streak += 1;
          state.mood = "HAPPY";
        } else {
          state.streak = 0;
          state.mood = "TOUGH_LOVE";
        }
        state.listeningMin = 0;
        state.speakingMin = 0;
        state.dailyGoalMet = false;
        state.lastLogin = today;
        saveState();
      }
    }

    function evaluateGoal() {
      if (state.listeningMin >= GOAL_LISTEN && state.speakingMin >= GOAL_SPEAK) {
        if (!state.dailyGoalMet) {
          state.dailyGoalMet = true;
          state.mood = "PROUD";
          setLine("CELEBRATION");
        }
      } else if (state.listeningMin > 0 && state.speakingMin === 0) {
        state.mood = "TOUGH_LOVE";
        setLine("TOUGH_LOVE");
      } else if (state.listeningMin === 0 && state.speakingMin === 0) {
        state.mood = "NEUTRAL";
      } else {
        state.mood = "HAPPY";
        if (Math.random() > 0.7) {
          setLine("NUDGE");
        }
      }
    }

    function renderUI() {
      // streak
      streakCountEl.textContent = state.streak.toString();

      // progress
      const listenPct = Math.min((state.listeningMin / GOAL_LISTEN) * 100, 100);
      const speakPct = Math.min((state.speakingMin / GOAL_SPEAK) * 100, 100);

      listenBarEl.style.width = listenPct + "%";
      speakBarEl.style.width = speakPct + "%";

      listenLabelEl.textContent = `${state.listeningMin} / ${GOAL_LISTEN}m`;
      speakLabelEl.textContent = `${state.speakingMin} / ${GOAL_SPEAK}m`;

      // mood + pulse
      chipMoodEl.textContent = `Mood: ${
        state.mood === "PROUD"
          ? "Proud"
          : state.mood === "TOUGH_LOVE"
          ? "Tough love"
          : state.mood === "HAPPY"
          ? "Positive"
          : "Neutral"
      }`;

      chipGoalEl.textContent = `Goal: ${state.dailyGoalMet ? "Complete" : "Not complete"}`;

      if (state.mood === "PROUD") {
        avatarEl.className = "avatar-core happy";
        pulseStatusEl.textContent =
          "You hit your goal. This is what real English training looks like.";
      } else if (state.mood === "TOUGH_LOVE") {
        avatarEl.className = "avatar-core slump";
        pulseStatusEl.textContent =
          "Streak broken or no speaking yet. I’m not angry, just honest. Let’s fix it today.";
      } else if (state.mood === "HAPPY") {
        avatarEl.className = "avatar-core happy";
        pulseStatusEl.textContent =
          "Good momentum. Keep stacking small wins – the tiger is waking up.";
      } else {
        avatarEl.className = "avatar-core";
        pulseStatusEl.textContent =
          "We start simple: 45 minutes listening, 15 minutes speaking. I’ll track the truth.";
      }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // EVENT HANDLERS
    // ========================================================================
    btnLogListen.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.listeningMin += 15;
      evaluateGoal();
      saveState();
    });

    btnLogSpeak.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.speakingMin += 5;
      evaluateGoal();
      saveState();
    });

    ctaLogToday.addEventListener("click", () => {
      // Scroll to tracker section
      const section = document.getElementById("section-tracker");
      section && section.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
      // gentle nudge line
      setLine("NUDGE");
    });

    ctaWhatCan.addEventListener("click", () => {
      const section = document.getElementById("section-capabilities");
      section && section.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    });

    // FAQ toggles
    faqItems.forEach(item => {
      const header = item.querySelector(".faq-header");
      header.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const isOpen = item.classList.contains("open");
        faqItems.forEach(i => i.classList.remove("open"));
        if (!isOpen) item.classList.add("open");
      });
    });

    // Listen to state:updated on EventBus (COMET-style pattern)
    EventBus.on("state:updated", renderUI);

    // ========================================================================
    // INIT
    // ========================================================================
    (function init() {
      checkDayRollover();
      renderUI();
      // initial greeting only if no goal yet for today
      if (!state.dailyGoalMet && state.listeningMin === 0 && state.speakingMin === 0) {
        setLine("GREETING");
      } else {
        evaluateGoal();
      }
      console.log("NET PET AI V1 :: ONLINE :: PHOENIX × COMET · GOSPEL 444 COMPLIANT");
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```
