```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <title>NET PET AI Coach — V1</title>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />

  <!-- Fonts -->
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
  <link
    href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap"
    rel="stylesheet"
  />

  <style>
    /* ===========================================================
       NET PET AI COACH — V1 (MOBILE FIRST)
       Gospel 444 Compliant — NO BLUE, Neon Purple + Gold + Deep Dark
       © Michael Hobbs — Executor
    ============================================================ */

    :root {
      --neon-purple: #a855f7;
      --neon-purple-deep: #7e22ce;
      --gold: #f59e0b;
      --gold-deep: #b45309;
      --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
      --card-bg: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.9);
      --glass-bg: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.85);
      --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
      --danger: #ef4444;
      --text-primary: #ffffff;
      --text-dim: rgba(229, 231, 235, 0.6);

      --radius-lg: 24px;
      --radius-full: 999px;

      --shadow-main: 0 18px 45px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
      --blur-glass: 16px;

      --transition-fast: 0.18s ease-out;
      --transition-base: 0.3s ease-out;
    }

    * {
      box-sizing: border-box;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
    }

    body {
      font-family: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont,
        sans-serif;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 10% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.25) 0%, transparent 60%),
                  radial-gradient(circle at 90% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25) 0%, transparent 60%),
                  var(--deep-dark);
      color: var(--text-primary);
      min-height: 100vh;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      padding: 16px;
    }

    .shell {
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 430px;
      height: 90vh;
      max-height: 820px;
      background: var(--card-bg);
      border-radius: 32px;
      border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
      box-shadow: var(--shadow-main);
      backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-glass));
      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-glass));
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      padding: 18px 18px 16px;
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    /* Ambient glow */
    .shell::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      inset: -40%;
      background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 15% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.25) 0%, transparent 50%),
        radial-gradient(circle at 85% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2) 0%, transparent 50%);
      opacity: 0.7;
      z-index: -1;
    }

    header {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      margin-bottom: 12px;
    }

    .brand-block {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
    }

    .logo-orb {
      width: 32px;
      height: 32px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--neon-purple), var(--neon-purple-deep));
      box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.7);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 18px;
    }

    .brand-text {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
      font-size: 11px;
      letter-spacing: 0.05em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
    }

    .brand-text span:first-child {
      color: var(--gold);
      font-weight: 700;
    }

    .brand-text span:last-child {
      color: var(--text-dim);
      font-weight: 400;
      font-size: 10px;
    }

    .meta {
      text-align: right;
      font-size: 11px;
    }

    .meta-top {
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .streak-pill {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
      padding: 3px 9px;
      border-radius: var(--radius-full);
      background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.16);
      color: var(--gold);
      font-weight: 700;
      margin-top: 4px;
      font-size: 11px;
    }

    .streak-pill span:first-child {
      font-size: 13px;
    }

    /* Main layout: avatar + message + trackers + actions */
    .main {
      flex: 1;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 14px;
      margin-top: 6px;
    }

    .avatar-zone {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 8px;
      margin-top: 4px;
    }

    .energy-wrapper {
      position: relative;
      width: 150px;
      height: 150px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
    }

    .energy-ring {
      position: absolute;
      width: 140px;
      height: 140px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      border: 2px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.45);
      animation: spin 14s linear infinite;
    }

    .energy-ring.inner {
      width: 120px;
      height: 120px;
      border-style: dashed;
      border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.6);
      animation-duration: 18s;
      animation-direction: reverse;
    }

    @keyframes spin {
      to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
    }

    .avatar-core {
      width: 96px;
      height: 96px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--neon-purple), var(--neon-purple-deep));
      box-shadow: 0 0 26px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.9);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 22px;
      transition: all var(--transition-base);
    }

    .avatar-core.mood-proud {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--gold), var(--gold-deep));
      box-shadow: 0 0 32px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.9);
      transform: scale(1.08);
    }

    .avatar-core.mood-slump {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, #7f1d1d, #450a0a);
      box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.7);
      opacity: 0.8;
    }

    .avatar-core.mood-tough {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--neon-purple-deep), #581c87);
      box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.8);
    }

    .energy-label {
      margin-top: 4px;
      font-size: 11px;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    }

    .energy-percent {
      font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
      font-size: 13px;
      color: var(--gold);
    }

    /* Dialogue */
    .message-box {
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
      border-radius: 14px;
      border-left: 4px solid var(--neon-purple);
      padding: 10px 12px;
      font-size: 13px;
      color: var(--text-primary);
      line-height: 1.5;
      position: relative;
      margin-top: 2px;
    }

    .message-box::before {
      content: '“';
      position: absolute;
      left: 8px;
      top: -8px;
      font-size: 26px;
      color: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.45);
    }

    .messageTone {
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.16em;
      margin-bottom: 2px;
    }

    /* Trackers */
    .trackers {
      margin-top: 4px;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 10px;
    }

    .tracker-row {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 4px;
    }

    .tracker-header {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      font-size: 11px;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    }

    .tracker-header span:last-child {
      font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
      color: var(--text-primary);
    }

    .bar-shell {
      width: 100%;
      height: 10px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .bar-fill {
      height: 100%;
      width: 0;
      border-radius: inherit;
      transition: width var(--transition-base);
    }

    .bar-fill.listen {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), #e9d5ff);
    }

    .bar-fill.speak {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--gold), #fef3c7);
    }

    /* Actions */
    .actions {
      margin-top: 6px;
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
      gap: 10px;
    }

    .btn {
      border: none;
      border-radius: 13px;
      padding: 10px 8px;
      font-size: 12px;
      font-weight: 600;
      letter-spacing: 0.04em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      cursor: pointer;
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
      color: var(--text-primary);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 3px;
      transition: transform var(--transition-fast), box-shadow var(--transition-fast), opacity var(--transition-fast);
    }

    .btn span.label-main {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 1;
    }

    .btn span.label-sub {
      font-size: 10px;
      font-weight: 400;
      opacity: 0.8;
      position: relative;
      z-index: 1;
    }

    .btn::before {
      content: '';
      position: absolute;
      inset: 0;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 0 0, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24), transparent 55%);
      opacity: 0;
      transition: opacity var(--transition-fast);
    }

    .btn:hover::before {
      opacity: 1;
    }

    .btn:hover {
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      box-shadow: 0 0 18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
    }

    .btn:active {
      transform: scale(0.97);
      opacity: 0.9;
    }

    .btn-listen {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--neon-purple), var(--neon-purple-deep));
      box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
    }

    .btn-speak {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--gold), var(--gold-deep));
      color: #111827;
      box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4);
    }

    .btn-reset {
      grid-column: span 2;
      background: transparent;
      border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18);
      color: var(--text-dim);
      font-size: 10px;
      padding: 6px 8px;
      box-shadow: none;
    }

    /* Reflection / mood toggles (no text input, just tap) */
    .reflection {
      margin-top: 4px;
      padding: 7px 9px;
      border-radius: 11px;
      background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
      border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3);
    }

    .reflection-title {
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.16em;
      margin-bottom: 5px;
    }

    .reflection-buttons {
      display: flex;
      gap: 6px;
    }

    .chip {
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 4px 8px;
      border: none;
      background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.9);
      color: var(--text-dim);
      font-size: 11px;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 4px;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast), color var(--transition-fast);
    }

    .chip:hover {
      background: rgba(55, 65, 81, 0.9);
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      color: var(--text-primary);
    }

    footer {
      margin-top: 6px;
      font-size: 10px;
      color: var(--text-dim);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
    }

    .tag-pill {
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.4);
      padding: 2px 8px;
      font-size: 9px;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.14em;
      color: var(--gold);
    }

    @media (max-width: 480px) {
      .shell {
        padding: 16px 14px 12px;
      }
      .energy-wrapper {
        width: 140px;
        height: 140px;
      }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="shell">
    <header>
      <div class="brand-block">
        <div class="logo-orb">🟣</div>
        <div class="brand-text">
          <span>NET PET AI</span>
          <span>daily english coach</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="meta">
        <div class="meta-top" id="meta-date">Today</div>
        <div class="streak-pill">
          <span>🔥</span>
          <span><span id="streakCount">0</span>d streak</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>

    <main class="main">
      <!-- Avatar / Energy -->
      <section class="avatar-zone">
        <div class="energy-wrapper">
          <div class="energy-ring"></div>
          <div class="energy-ring inner"></div>
          <div class="avatar-core" id="avatarCore">🐯</div>
        </div>
        <div class="energy-label">
          English Energy: <span class="energy-percent" id="energyPercent">0%</span>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- Message -->
      <section class="message-box">
        <div class="messageTone" id="messageTone">MODE: GREETING</div>
        <div id="messageText">
          Initializing neural link... building your daily plan.
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- Trackers -->
      <section class="trackers">
        <div class="tracker-row">
          <div class="tracker-header">
            <span>Listening Input</span>
            <span id="listenStats">0 / 45m</span>
          </div>
          <div class="bar-shell">
            <div class="bar-fill listen" id="barListen"></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="tracker-row">
          <div class="tracker-header">
            <span>Speaking / Mimic</span>
            <span id="speakStats">0 / 15m</span>
          </div>
          <div class="bar-shell">
            <div class="bar-fill speak" id="barSpeak"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- Actions -->
      <section class="actions">
        <button class="btn btn-listen" id="btnListen">
          <span class="label-main">+15m Listening</span>
          <span class="label-sub">sitcom / podcast / youtube</span>
        </button>
        <button class="btn btn-speak" id="btnSpeak">
          <span class="label-main">+5m Speaking</span>
          <span class="label-sub">shadow + record</span>
        </button>
        <button class="btn btn-reset" id="btnReset">
          Reset today (debug) — not for real students 😅
        </button>
      </section>

      <!-- Reflection (no free text, just toggles) -->
      <section class="reflection">
        <div class="reflection-title">how are you feeling about english today?</div>
        <div class="reflection-buttons">
          <button class="chip" id="chipOnFire">🔥 On fire</button>
          <button class="chip" id="chipTired">😴 Tired</button>
          <button class="chip" id="chipNoTime">⏰ No time</button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>

    <footer>
      <span class="tag-pill">45 / 15 • 60% rule • tiger voice</span>
      <span style="opacity: 0.7;">v1.0</span>
    </footer>
  </div>

  <script>
    'use strict';

    /**
     * NET PET AI COACH — V1
     * Phoenix Engine Mini-Core
     * State = streak, mins, dailyGoal, mood, lastLogin
     */

    const STORAGE_KEY = 'netPetStateV1';

    const defaultState = {
      streak: 0,
      listeningMin: 0,
      speakingMin: 0,
      dailyGoalMet: false,
      lastLogin: null, // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
      mood: 'NEUTRAL'  // NEUTRAL | PROGRESS | TOUGH_LOVE | PROUD | SLUMP
    };

    let state = loadState();

    // Voice Matrix (Claude-style, your tones)
    const VOICE_MATRIX = {
      GREETING: [
        "Let's get to work. Consistency beats intensity.",
        "Welcome back. Today we kill the robot voice.",
        "We learn languages by listening, mimicking, and showing up.",
        "You don't need perfect grammar. You need daily contact."
      ],
      TOUGH_LOVE: [
        "You can't learn to swim by reading a book. Speak.",
        "If you avoid mistakes, you avoid progress.",
        "I will push your comfort zone. That's why I'm here.",
        "Do you want fluency, or do you want excuses?"
      ],
      CELEBRATION: [
        "Boom. That's Tiger Energy right there.",
        "This is how fluent people are built — one boring day at a time.",
        "You're not 'studying' English anymore. You're living with it.",
        "Proud of you. Tomorrow, we repeat."
      ],
      NUDGE: [
        "Just one more mini session. 5 minutes. You have it.",
        "Pick one sitcom, episode 1. Press play. No pausing.",
        "Mimic the emotion, not just the words.",
        "Remember: 60% understanding is enough. Let the rest slide."
      ],
      SLUMP: [
        "We broke the streak. It hurts. Good. Use that.",
        "Yesterday is gone. Today is 100% fresh. Start with 15 minutes.",
        "The prison is in your mind, not in your schedule.",
        "Don't disappear. Mini session. Right now."
      ]
    };

    // Helpers
    function todayKey() {
      const d = new Date();
      const y = d.getFullYear();
      const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
      const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
      return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
    }

    function loadState() {
      try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!raw) {
          const fresh = { ...defaultState, lastLogin: todayKey() };
          localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(fresh));
          return fresh;
        }
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);

        // Cheap migration safety
        return {
          ...defaultState,
          ...parsed
        };
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('NET PET AI: State corrupted. Resetting.', e);
        const fresh = { ...defaultState, lastLogin: todayKey() };
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(fresh));
        return fresh;
      }
    }

    function saveState() {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
    }

    // Mood & message logic
    function pickRandom(arr) {
      return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
    }

    function speak(mode) {
      const messageText = document.getElementById('messageText');
      const messageTone = document.getElementById('messageTone');

      const upper = mode.toUpperCase();
      const bank = VOICE_MATRIX[upper];
      if (!bank) return;

      messageTone.textContent = `MODE: ${upper.replace('_', ' ')}`;
      messageText.textContent = pickRandom(bank);
    }

    function updateMoodAndMessage(trigger) {
      const totalL = state.listeningMin;
      const totalS = state.speakingMin;

      // Goal check: 45m listen + 15m speak
      const goalHit = totalL >= 45 && totalS >= 15;

      if (goalHit && !state.dailyGoalMet) {
        state.dailyGoalMet = true;
        state.mood = 'PROUD';
        speak('CELEBRATION');
      } else if (state.mood === 'SLUMP' && trigger === 'init') {
        // Keep slump line on init
        speak('SLUMP');
      } else if (totalL === 0 && totalS === 0) {
        state.mood = 'NEUTRAL';
        if (trigger === 'init') {
          speak('GREETING');
        }
      } else if (totalL > 0 && totalS === 0) {
        state.mood = 'TOUGH_LOVE';
        if (trigger !== 'quiet') speak('TOUGH_LOVE');
      } else {
        state.mood = 'PROGRESS';
        if (Math.random() > 0.65 && trigger !== 'quiet') {
          speak('NUDGE');
        }
      }

      updateAvatar();
      saveState();
      render();
    }

    function updateAvatar() {
      const avatar = document.getElementById('avatarCore');
      avatar.className = 'avatar-core';

      if (state.mood === 'PROUD') {
        avatar.classList.add('mood-proud');
        avatar.textContent = '🏆';
      } else if (state.mood === 'SLUMP') {
        avatar.classList.add('mood-slump');
        avatar.textContent = '💤';
      } else if (state.mood === 'TOUGH_LOVE') {
        avatar.classList.add('mood-tough');
        avatar.textContent = '🗡️';
      } else if (state.mood === 'PROGRESS') {
        avatar.textContent = '🐯';
      } else {
        avatar.textContent = '🐯';
      }
    }

    // Energy calculation
    function computeEnergyPercent() {
      const listenRatio = Math.min(state.listeningMin / 45, 1);
      const speakRatio = Math.min(state.speakingMin / 15, 1);
      // Weighted slightly toward listening, as per canon
      const combined = listenRatio * 0.7 + speakRatio * 0.3;
      return Math.round(combined * 100);
    }

    function render() {
      const streakEl = document.getElementById('streakCount');
      const listenStats = document.getElementById('listenStats');
      const speakStats = document.getElementById('speakStats');
      const barListen = document.getElementById('barListen');
      const barSpeak = document.getElementById('barSpeak');
      const energyPercentEl = document.getElementById('energyPercent');
      const metaDate = document.getElementById('meta-date');

      streakEl.textContent = state.streak;
      listenStats.textContent = `${state.listeningMin} / 45m`;
      speakStats.textContent = `${state.speakingMin} / 15m`;

      const listenPct = Math.min((state.listeningMin / 45) * 100, 100);
      const speakPct = Math.min((state.speakingMin / 15) * 100, 100);
      barListen.style.width = `${listenPct}%`;
      barSpeak.style.width = `${speakPct}%`;

      const energy = computeEnergyPercent();
      energyPercentEl.textContent = `${energy}%`;

      const todayLabel = new Date().toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
        weekday: 'short',
        month: 'short',
        day: 'numeric'
      });
      metaDate.textContent = todayLabel;
    }

    // Decay / streak logic
    function applyDecay() {
      const today = todayKey();
      const last = state.lastLogin || today;
      if (today === last) return;

      const lastDate = new Date(last);
      const todayDate = new Date(today);
      const diffMs = todayDate - lastDate;
      const diffDays = Math.round(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));

      if (diffDays >= 1) {
        // If they didn't meet yesterday's goal, streak breaks
        if (!state.dailyGoalMet) {
          state.streak = 0;
          state.mood = 'SLUMP';
          speak('SLUMP');
        } else {
          state.streak += 1;
          state.mood = 'PROGRESS';
        }

        // New day: reset counters & daily flag
        state.listeningMin = 0;
        state.speakingMin = 0;
        state.dailyGoalMet = false;
      }

      state.lastLogin = today;
      saveState();
    }

    // Event listeners
    document.getElementById('btnListen').addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.listeningMin += 15;
      if (state.listeningMin > 180) state.listeningMin = 180; // Hard cap for a day
      updateMoodAndMessage('action');
    });

    document.getElementById('btnSpeak').addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.speakingMin += 5;
      if (state.speakingMin > 90) state.speakingMin = 90;
      updateMoodAndMessage('action');
    });

    document.getElementById('btnReset').addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.listeningMin = 0;
      state.speakingMin = 0;
      state.dailyGoalMet = false;
      state.mood = 'NEUTRAL';
      saveState();
      speak('GREETING');
      render();
    });

    // Reflection chips (mood / message only, no storage)
    document.getElementById('chipOnFire').addEventListener('click', () => {
      // gentle celebration
      state.mood = 'PROGRESS';
      speak('CELEBRATION');
      updateAvatar();
    });

    document.getElementById('chipTired').addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.mood = 'TOUGH_LOVE';
      speak('TOUGH_LOVE');
      updateAvatar();
    });

    document.getElementById('chipNoTime').addEventListener('click', () => {
      state.mood = 'TOUGH_LOVE';
      speak('SLUMP');
      updateAvatar();
    });

    // Init
    (function init() {
      applyDecay();
      updateAvatar();
      updateMoodAndMessage('init'); // sets initial line
      render();
      console.log('NET PET AI v1 — Online • Gospel 444 Compliant • 45/15 Tracker Active');
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```


Here’s your **mobile app version** of NET PET AI Coach – same brain, but laid out like a real phone app.

Save this as **`net-pet-ai-mobile.html`** and open it on your phone (or via your HTML Viewer). It’s 100% Vanilla JS, Gospel 444 compliant, no inline events, no blue, touch-friendly.

---

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <title>NET PET AI Coach — Mobile</title>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover" />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#0f0f1a" />
  <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-capable" content="yes" />
  <meta name="apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style" content="black-translucent" />
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />

  <style>
    :root {
      --neon-purple: #a855f7;
      --gold-accent: #f59e0b;
      --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
      --glass-bg: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.7);
      --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
      --text-primary: #f9fafb;
      --text-dim: rgba(249, 250, 251, 0.65);
      --danger: #ef4444;
      --success: #22c55e;
    }

    * {
      box-sizing: border-box;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
    }

    html, body {
      height: 100%;
      width: 100%;
      background-color: var(--deep-dark);
      font-family: "Inter", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
      color: var(--text-primary);
    }

    body {
      display: flex;
      align-items: stretch;
      justify-content: center;
      overflow: hidden;
      position: relative;
      padding: env(safe-area-inset-top) env(safe-area-inset-right) env(safe-area-inset-bottom) env(safe-area-inset-left);
    }

    /* Ambient Background */
    .ambient {
      position: fixed;
      inset: 0;
      pointer-events: none;
      z-index: 0;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .ambient-blob {
      position: absolute;
      width: 480px;
      height: 480px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(168,85,247,0.22) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 70%);
      filter: blur(6px);
      top: 60%;
      left: 50%;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      animation: breathe 10s ease-in-out infinite;
    }

    @keyframes breathe {
      0%, 100% { opacity: 0.4; transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1); }
      50% { opacity: 0.85; transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1.06); }
    }

    /* APP SHELL (phone frame feel) */
    .app-shell {
      position: relative;
      z-index: 1;
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 480px;
      height: 100vh;
      max-height: 900px;
      margin: auto;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      padding: 12px;
    }

    .app-card {
      flex: 1;
      background: var(--glass-bg);
      backdrop-filter: blur(18px);
      -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(18px);
      border-radius: 28px;
      border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
      padding: 16px 16px 8px 16px;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      box-shadow:
        0 18px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.8),
        0 0 40px rgba(168,85,247,0.45);
    }

    /* Header */
    .header {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      margin-bottom: 10px;
    }

    .header-left {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 2px;
    }

    .app-title {
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      letter-spacing: 0.08em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      font-weight: 700;
      color: var(--gold-accent);
      font-family: "JetBrains Mono", monospace;
    }

    .app-subtitle {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .streak-pill {
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
      padding: 4px 10px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(245,158,11,0.16);
      color: var(--gold-accent);
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    .streak-pill span {
      font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    }

    /* Avatar + Today summary row */
    .top-row {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1.2fr 1.4fr;
      gap: 12px;
      align-items: stretch;
      margin-bottom: 10px;
      margin-top: 4px;
    }

    .avatar-wrap {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      position: relative;
      padding: 4px;
    }

    .avatar-orbit {
      position: absolute;
      width: 120px;
      height: 120px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      border: 2px solid rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
      animation: spin 12s linear infinite;
    }

    .avatar-orbit.inner {
      width: 94px;
      height: 94px;
      border-style: dashed;
      border-color: rgba(245,158,11,0.6);
      animation-duration: 18s;
      animation-direction: reverse;
    }

    @keyframes spin {
      to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
    }

    .avatar-core {
      width: 84px;
      height: 84px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--neon-purple), #4c1d95);
      box-shadow: 0 0 26px var(--neon-purple);
      transition: all 0.45s ease;
    }

    .avatar-core.happy {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--gold-accent), #b45309);
      box-shadow: 0 0 40px var(--gold-accent);
    }

    .avatar-core.slump {
      background: radial-gradient(circle, #991b1b, #450a0a);
      box-shadow: 0 0 18px var(--danger);
      opacity: 0.8;
    }

    .today-card {
      border-radius: 18px;
      padding: 10px 12px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at top left, rgba(168,85,247,0.22), rgba(15,15,26,0.95));
      border: 1px solid rgba(248,250,252,0.06);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      justify-content: space-between;
      gap: 6px;
    }

    .today-label {
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.08em;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .today-main {
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    .today-tags {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: 4px;
      margin-top: 2px;
    }

    .tag {
      font-size: 0.65rem;
      padding: 3px 7px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.8);
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.4);
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .tag-strong {
      border-color: rgba(245,158,11,0.8);
      color: var(--gold-accent);
      background: rgba(245,158,11,0.08);
    }

    /* Dialogue */
    .dialogue {
      margin-top: 8px;
      margin-bottom: 10px;
      border-radius: 14px;
      padding: 10px 12px;
      background: rgba(0,0,0,0.32);
      border-left: 3px solid var(--neon-purple);
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      line-height: 1.5;
      min-height: 64px;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
    }

    .dialogue em {
      font-style: italic;
      color: var(--text-primary);
    }

    /* Trackers */
    .trackers {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 10px;
      margin-bottom: 10px;
      margin-top: 4px;
    }

    .tracker-row {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 4px;
    }

    .tracker-info {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: baseline;
    }

    .tracker-label {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      font-weight: 500;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.08em;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .tracker-value {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .progress-bg {
      width: 100%;
      height: 10px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.9);
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .progress-fill {
      height: 100%;
      border-radius: 999px;
      transition: width 0.45s ease;
    }

    .fill-listen {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), #d8b4fe);
    }

    .fill-speak {
      background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--gold-accent), #facc15);
    }

    /* Bottom Action Bar */
    .bottom-bar {
      margin-top: auto;
      padding-top: 8px;
      border-top: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.25);
    }

    .primary-actions {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
      gap: 10px;
      margin-bottom: 6px;
    }

    .btn {
      appearance: none;
      border: none;
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 14px 10px;
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.06em;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: transform 0.12s ease, box-shadow 0.12s ease;
      font-family: "Inter", system-ui;
    }

    .btn:active {
      transform: scale(0.97);
      box-shadow: none;
    }

    .btn-listen {
      background: var(--neon-purple);
      color: #f9fafb;
      box-shadow: 0 8px 16px rgba(168,85,247,0.35);
    }

    .btn-speak {
      background: var(--gold-accent);
      color: var(--deep-dark);
      box-shadow: 0 8px 16px rgba(245,158,11,0.4);
    }

    .bottom-meta {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      color: var(--text-dim);
    }

    .pill-soft {
      padding: 4px 8px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px dashed rgba(148,163,184,0.6);
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      font-family: "JetBrains Mono", monospace;
    }

    .debug-btn {
      background: transparent;
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.35);
      color: var(--text-dim);
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 4px 8px;
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      cursor: pointer;
    }

    .debug-btn:active {
      transform: scale(0.97);
    }

    @media (max-height: 720px) {
      .avatar-core {
        width: 72px;
        height: 72px;
      }
      .avatar-orbit {
        width: 104px;
        height: 104px;
      }
      .avatar-orbit.inner {
        width: 86px;
        height: 86px;
      }
      .today-card {
        padding: 8px 10px;
      }
      .dialogue {
        min-height: 56px;
        font-size: 0.85rem;
      }
    }

  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="ambient">
    <div class="ambient-blob"></div>
  </div>

  <main class="app-shell">
    <section class="app-card" aria-label="NET PET AI Coach">
      <header class="header">
        <div class="header-left">
          <div class="app-title">NET PET AI v1.0</div>
          <div class="app-subtitle">Your daily English discipline coach</div>
        </div>
        <div class="streak-pill">
          🔥 <span id="streak-display">0</span> day streak
        </div>
      </header>

      <section class="top-row" aria-label="Avatar and today summary">
        <div class="avatar-wrap">
          <div class="avatar-orbit"></div>
          <div class="avatar-orbit inner"></div>
          <div class="avatar-core" id="pet-avatar" aria-hidden="true"></div>
        </div>

        <article class="today-card">
          <div class="today-label">Today’s Mission</div>
          <div class="today-main" id="today-main-text">
            45 minutes listening, 15 minutes speaking.
          </div>
          <div class="today-tags" id="today-tags">
            <span class="tag tag-strong">No grammar prison</span>
            <span class="tag">Make it fun</span>
            <span class="tag">Don’t pause</span>
          </div>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section class="dialogue" aria-live="polite">
        <p id="ai-message"><em>Initializing neural link...</em></p>
      </section>

      <section class="trackers" aria-label="Daily English progress">
        <div class="tracker-row">
          <div class="tracker-info">
            <span class="tracker-label">Listening Input</span>
            <span class="tracker-value" id="listen-stats">0 / 45m</span>
          </div>
          <div class="progress-bg">
            <div class="progress-fill fill-listen" id="bar-listen" style="width: 0%"></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="tracker-row">
          <div class="tracker-info">
            <span class="tracker-label">Speaking / Mimic</span>
            <span class="tracker-value" id="speak-stats">0 / 15m</span>
          </div>
          <div class="progress-bg">
            <div class="progress-fill fill-speak" id="bar-speak" style="width: 0%"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <footer class="bottom-bar">
        <div class="primary-actions">
          <button class="btn btn-listen" id="btn-log-listen">
            +15m Listening
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-speak" id="btn-log-speak">
            +5m Speaking
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="bottom-meta">
          <div class="pill-soft">
            60% Rule · 45/15 Lifestyle
          </div>
          <button class="debug-btn" id="btn-reset" type="button">
            Reset today (debug)
          </button>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </section>
  </main>

  <script>
    /**
     * NET PET AI COACH — MOBILE VERSION
     * Phoenix Engine · Gospel 444 · Canon Voice
     * No inline events. No unsafe HTML. LocalStorage state.
     */

    const defaultState = {
      streak: 0,
      listeningMin: 0,
      speakingMin: 0,
      lastLogin: new Date().toDateString(),
      mood: "NEUTRAL", // NEUTRAL, HAPPY, TOUGH_LOVE, PROUD
      dailyGoalMet: false
    };

    let state;
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem("netPetState");
      state = saved ? { ...defaultState, ...JSON.parse(saved) } : { ...defaultState };
    } catch (e) {
      state = { ...defaultState };
    }

    const VOICE_MATRIX = {
      GREETING: [
        "Let's get to work. Consistency beats intensity.",
        "Welcome back. Ready to break the grammar prison?",
        "The robot voice ends today. Let's speak like humans.",
        "Don't be a mouse. Be a tiger today."
      ],
      TOUGH_LOVE: [
        "You haven't practiced speaking yet. You can't learn to swim by reading a book.",
        "Grammar won't save you in a meeting. Confidence will. Speak up.",
        "I will push your comfort zone because that's the only way to get results.",
        "Do you want to be fluent, or do you just want to say you're 'studying'?"
      ],
      CELEBRATION: [
        "Boom! That's the Tiger Energy I'm talking about!",
        "Daily habit unlocked. This is how you win.",
        "You're not studying English anymore. You're living it.",
        "Outstanding. Tomorrow, we do it again."
      ],
      NUDGE: [
        "45 minutes of listening isn't hard if you watch something fun.",
        "Just 5 more minutes of shadowing. Mimic the emotion, not just the words.",
        "Remember: Understand 60%, ignore the rest. Keep the flow.",
        "Don't pause. Real life doesn't have a pause button."
      ]
    };

    const elements = {
      streakDisplay: document.getElementById("streak-display"),
      listenStats: document.getElementById("listen-stats"),
      speakStats: document.getElementById("speak-stats"),
      barListen: document.getElementById("bar-listen"),
      barSpeak: document.getElementById("bar-speak"),
      aiMessage: document.getElementById("ai-message"),
      avatar: document.getElementById("pet-avatar"),
      todayMain: document.getElementById("today-main-text"),
      btnLogListen: document.getElementById("btn-log-listen"),
      btnLogSpeak: document.getElementById("btn-log-speak"),
      btnReset: document.getElementById("btn-reset")
    };

    function saveState() {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("netPetState", JSON.stringify(state));
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("NET PET AI: Unable to save state", e);
      }
      renderUI();
    }

    function speak(category) {
      const options = VOICE_MATRIX[category];
      if (!options || options.length === 0) return;
      const msg = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
      elements.aiMessage.textContent = `"${msg}"`;
    }

    function checkDecay() {
      const today = new Date().toDateString();
      if (state.lastLogin !== today) {
        if (!state.dailyGoalMet && (state.listeningMin > 0 || state.speakingMin > 0)) {
          state.streak = 0;
          state.mood = "TOUGH_LOVE";
        } else if (state.dailyGoalMet) {
          state.streak += 1;
          state.mood = "HAPPY";
        } else {
          state.mood = "NEUTRAL";
        }
        state.listeningMin = 0;
        state.speakingMin = 0;
        state.dailyGoalMet = false;
        state.lastLogin = today;
        saveState();
      }
    }

    function updateAvatar() {
      const avatar = elements.avatar;
      avatar.className = "avatar-core";

      if (state.mood === "PROUD") {
        avatar.classList.add("happy");
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--neon-purple", "#f59e0b");
      } else if (state.mood === "TOUGH_LOVE") {
        avatar.classList.add("slump");
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--neon-purple", "#a855f7");
      } else if (state.mood === "HAPPY") {
        avatar.classList.add("happy");
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--neon-purple", "#a855f7");
      } else {
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--neon-purple", "#a855f7");
      }
    }

    function updateMoodAndVoice() {
      const totalListen = state.listeningMin;
      const totalSpeak = state.speakingMin;

      if (totalListen >= 45 && totalSpeak >= 15) {
        if (!state.dailyGoalMet) {
          state.dailyGoalMet = true;
          state.mood = "PROUD";
          speak("CELEBRATION");
        } else {
          state.mood = "HAPPY";
        }
      } else if (totalListen === 0 && totalSpeak === 0) {
        state.mood = "NEUTRAL";
      } else if (totalListen > 0 && totalSpeak === 0) {
        state.mood = "TOUGH_LOVE";
        speak("TOUGH_LOVE");
      } else {
        state.mood = "HAPPY";
        if (Math.random() > 0.7) speak("NUDGE");
      }

      updateAvatar();
      updateTodayText();
    }

    function updateTodayText() {
      const l = state.listeningMin;
      const s = state.speakingMin;
      const t = elements.todayMain;

      if (l === 0 && s === 0) {
        t.textContent = "Start small: 15 minutes listening, 5 minutes speaking.";
        return;
      }
      if (l < 45 && s < 15) {
        t.textContent = "Good start. Keep going until you hit 45 / 15 today.";
        return;
      }
      if (l >= 45 && s < 15) {
        t.textContent = "Listening done. Now it's time to be a tiger and speak.";
        return;
      }
      if (l < 45 && s >= 15) {
        t.textContent = "Speaking is strong. Finish the listening mission.";
        return;
      }
      if (l >= 45 && s >= 15) {
        t.textContent = "Mission complete. Now just live your life in English.";
      }
    }

    function renderUI() {
      elements.streakDisplay.textContent = state.streak.toString();

      const listenPct = Math.min((state.listeningMin / 45) * 100, 100);
      const speakPct = Math.min((state.speakingMin / 15) * 100, 100);

      elements.barListen.style.width = listenPct + "%";
      elements.barSpeak.style.width = speakPct + "%";

      elements.listenStats.textContent = `${state.listeningMin} / 45m`;
      elements.speakStats.textContent = `${state.speakingMin} / 15m`;

      updateAvatar();
      updateTodayText();
    }

    elements.btnLogListen.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.listeningMin += 15;
      updateMoodAndVoice();
      saveState();
    });

    elements.btnLogSpeak.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.speakingMin += 5;
      updateMoodAndVoice();
      saveState();
    });

    elements.btnReset.addEventListener("click", () => {
      state.listeningMin = 0;
      state.speakingMin = 0;
      state.dailyGoalMet = false;
      state.mood = "NEUTRAL";
      speak("GREETING");
      saveState();
    });

    (function init() {
      checkDecay();
      renderUI();
      if (!state.listeningMin && !state.speakingMin && !state.dailyGoalMet) {
        speak("GREETING");
      } else {
        // Soft nudge on reload if mid-day
        if (Math.random() > 0.5) {
          speak("NUDGE");
        }
      }
      console.log("NET PET AI Coach (Mobile) :: ONLINE :: Gospel 444 Compliant");
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>NET PET AI — Your Digital Coach</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

    <style>
        /* GOSPEL 444 AESTHETICS — CANON STRICT */
        :root {
            --neon-purple: #a855f7;
            --gold-accent: #f59e0b;
            --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --glass-bg: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.6);
            --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --text-primary: #ffffff;
            --text-dim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
            --danger: #ef4444;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--deep-dark);
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            color: var(--text-primary);
            height: 100vh;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            overflow: hidden;
            position: relative;
        }

        .ambient-light {
            position: absolute;
            width: 600px;
            height: 600px;
            background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(168,85,247,0.15) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 70%);
            top: 50%;
            left: 50%;
            transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
            animation: breathe 8s infinite ease-in-out;
            z-index: 0;
        }

        @keyframes breathe {
            0%,100% { opacity: 0.5; transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1); }
            50% { opacity: 0.8; transform: translate(-50%, -50%) scale(1.1); }
        }

        .pet-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 420px;
            height: 90vh;
            max-height: 800px;
            background: var(--glass-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: 32px;
            padding: 24px;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            z-index: 10;
            position: relative;
            box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
        }

        .header {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            padding-bottom: 15px;
        }

        .brand {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: var(--gold-accent);
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            font-size: 0.9rem;
        }

        .streak-badge {
            background: rgba(245,158,11,0.2);
            color: var(--gold-accent);
            padding: 4px 12px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 5px;
        }

        .avatar-container {
            flex: 1;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            align-items: center;
            position: relative;
        }

        .avatar-core {
            width: 120px;
            height: 120px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--neon-purple), #4c1d95);
            box-shadow: 0 0 30px var(--neon-purple);
            z-index: 2;
            position: relative;
            transition: all 0.5s ease;
        }

        .avatar-ring {
            position: absolute;
            width: 160px;
            height: 160px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            border: 2px solid rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
            animation: spin 10s linear infinite;
        }

        .avatar-ring.inner {
            width: 140px;
            height: 140px;
            border: 1px dashed rgba(245,158,11,0.5);
            animation: spin 15s linear infinite reverse;
        }

        @keyframes spin {
            100% { transform: rotate(360deg); }
        }

        .avatar-core.happy { 
            box-shadow: 0 0 50px var(--gold-accent);
            background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--gold-accent), #b45309);
        }

        .avatar-core.slump { 
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--danger);
            background: radial-gradient(circle, #991b1b, #450a0a);
            opacity: 0.7;
        }

        .dialogue-box {
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            border-left: 4px solid var(--neon-purple);
            padding: 15px;
            border-radius: 0 12px 12px 0;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            min-height: 80px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
        }

        .dialogue-text {
            font-size: 1rem;
            line-height: 1.5;
            font-style: italic;
        }

        .trackers {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 15px;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }

        .tracker-label {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
        }

        .progress-bg {
            width: 100%;
            height: 12px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            border-radius: 6px;
            overflow: hidden;
        }

        .progress-fill {
            height: 100%;
            border-radius: 6px;
            transition: width 0.5s ease;
        }

        .fill-listening { background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), #d8b4fe); }
        .fill-speaking  { background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--gold-accent), #fcd34d); }

        .actions {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
            gap: 12px;
        }

        .btn {
            padding: 14px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            font-weight: 600;
            border: none;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: 0.2s;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            font-size: 0.85rem;
        }

        .btn-listen {
            background: var(--neon-purple);
            color: white;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(168,85,247,0.3);
        }

        .btn-speak {
            background: var(--gold-accent);
            color: var(--deep-dark);
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(245,158,11,0.3);
        }

        .btn-reset {
            grid-column: span 2;
            background: transparent;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
            color: var(--text-dim);
            font-size: 0.7rem;
            padding: 8px;
        }
    </style>
</head>

<body>

<div class="ambient-light"></div>

<div class="pet-container">
    <div class="header">
        <div class="brand">NET PET AI v1.0</div>
        <div class="streak-badge">🔥 <span id="streak-display">0</span> Day Streak</div>
    </div>

    <div class="avatar-container">
        <div class="avatar-ring"></div>
        <div class="avatar-ring inner"></div>
        <div class="avatar-core" id="pet-avatar"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="dialogue-box">
        <div class="dialogue-text" id="ai-message">Initializing neural link...</div>
    </div>

    <div class="trackers">
        <div>
            <div class="tracker-label">
                <span>Listening Input</span>
                <span id="listen-stats">0 / 45m</span>
            </div>
            <div class="progress-bg">
                <div class="progress-fill fill-listening" id="bar-listen" style="width: 0%"></div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div>
            <div class="tracker-label">
                <span>Speaking / Mimic</span>
                <span id="speak-stats">0 / 15m</span>
            </div>
            <div class="progress-bg">
                <div class="progress-fill fill-speaking" id="bar-speak" style="width: 0%"></div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="actions">
        <button class="btn btn-listen" id="btn-log-listen">+15m Listening</button>
        <button class="btn btn-speak" id="btn-log-speak">+5m Speaking</button>
        <button class="btn btn-reset" id="btn-reset">Reset Daily Progress</button>
    </div>

</div>

<script>
    /* PHOENIX ENGINE — NET PET AI CORE */

    const defaultState = {
        streak: 0,
        listeningMin: 0,
        speakingMin: 0,
        lastLogin: new Date().toDateString(),
        mood: 'NEUTRAL',
        dailyGoalMet: false
    };

    let state = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('netPetState')) || defaultState;

    const VOICE_MATRIX = {
        GREETING: [
            "Let's get to work. Consistency beats intensity.",
            "Welcome back. Ready to break the grammar prison?",
            "The robot voice ends today. Let's speak like humans.",
            "Don't be a mouse. Be a tiger today."
        ],
        TOUGH_LOVE: [
            "You haven't practiced speaking yet. You can't learn to swim by reading a book.",
            "Grammar won't save you in a meeting. Confidence will. Speak up.",
            "I will push your comfort zone because that’s the only way to get results.",
            "Do you want to be fluent, or do you just want to say you're studying?"
        ],
        CELEBRATION: [
            "Boom! That's the Tiger Energy I'm talking about!",
            "Daily habit unlocked. This is how you win.",
            "You're not studying English anymore. You're living it.",
            "Outstanding. Tomorrow, we do it again."
        ],
        NUDGE: [
            "45 minutes of listening isn't hard if you watch something fun.",
            "Just 5 more minutes of shadowing. Mimic the emotion, not just the words.",
            "Remember: Understand 60%, ignore the rest. Keep the flow.",
            "Don't pause. Real life doesn't have a pause button."
        ]
    };

    function saveState() {
        localStorage.setItem('netPetState', JSON.stringify(state));
        renderUI();
    }

    function checkDecay() {
        const today = new Date().toDateString();

        if (state.lastLogin !== today) {
            if (!state.dailyGoalMet) {
                state.streak = 0;
                state.mood = 'TOUGH_LOVE';
            } else {
                state.streak += 1;
                state.mood = 'HAPPY';
            }

            state.listeningMin = 0;
            state.speakingMin = 0;
            state.dailyGoalMet = false;
            state.lastLogin = today;
            saveState();
        }
    }

    function updateMood() {
        const totalListen = state.listeningMin;
        const totalSpeak = state.speakingMin;

        if (totalListen >= 45 && totalSpeak >= 15) {
            if (!state.dailyGoalMet) {
                state.dailyGoalMet = true;
                state.mood = 'PROUD';
                speak('CELEBRATION');
            }
        } else if (totalListen === 0 && totalSpeak === 0) {
            state.mood = 'NEUTRAL';
        } else if (totalListen > 0 && totalSpeak === 0) {
            state.mood = 'TOUGH_LOVE';
            speak('TOUGH_LOVE');
        } else {
            state.mood = 'HAPPY';
            if (Math.random() > 0.7) speak('NUDGE');
        }

        updateAvatar();
    }

    function speak(category) {
        const options = VOICE_MATRIX[category];
        const msg = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
        document.getElementById('ai-message').textContent = `"${msg}"`;
    }

    function updateAvatar() {
        const avatar = document.getElementById('pet-avatar');

        avatar.className = 'avatar-core';

        if (state.mood === 'PROUD') {
            avatar.classList.add('happy');
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--neon-purple', '#f59e0b');
        } else if (state.mood === 'TOUGH_LOVE') {
            avatar.classList.add('slump');
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--neon-purple', '#a855f7');
        } else {
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--neon-purple', '#a855f7');
        }
    }

    function renderUI() {
        document.getElementById('streak-display').textContent = state.streak;

        const listenPct = Math.min((state.listeningMin / 45) * 100, 100);
        const speakPct = Math.min((state.speakingMin / 15) * 100, 100);

        document.getElementById('bar-listen').style.width = `${listenPct}%`;
        document.getElementById('bar-speak').style.width = `${speakPct}%`;

        document.getElementById('listen-stats').textContent = `${state.listeningMin} / 45m`;
        document.getElementById('speak-stats').textContent = `${state.speakingMin} / 15m`;
    }

    document.getElementById('btn-log-listen').addEventListener('click', () => {
        state.listeningMin += 15;
        updateMood();
        saveState();
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-log-speak').addEventListener('click', () => {
        state.speakingMin += 5;
        updateMood();
        saveState();
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-reset').addEventListener('click', () => {
        state.listeningMin = 0;
        state.speakingMin = 0;
        state.dailyGoalMet = false;
        state.mood = 'NEUTRAL';
        speak('GREETING');
        saveState();
    });

    (function init() {
        checkDecay();
        renderUI();
        updateAvatar();
        speak('GREETING');
    })();

</script>

</body>
</html>
```

## 5. Core State & Storage

### `state.js`

```js
// src/core/state.js
import { STUDENTS } from "../data/students.js";
import { LESSONS } from "../data/lessons.js";

export const NET_STATE = {
  version: "V1.5",
  activeView: "dashboard", // 'buddy', 'lessons', 'session', 'universe'
  selectedStudentId: null,
  selectedLessonId: null,
  buddies: STUDENTS,
  lessons: LESSONS,
  session: {
    locked: false,
    buddyId: null,
    topic: "",
    minutes: 30,
    notes: "",
  },

    dailyListeningMinutes: 0,
    dailySpeakingMinutes: 0,
    confidenceScore: 0.4, // 0–1
  },
};
```

### `storage.js`

```js
// src/core/storage.js
import { NET_STATE } from "./state.js";
import { hashState } from "../security/hash.js";

const STORAGE_KEY = "NET_APP_STATE_V1";

export function loadState() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return;

    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    // Optional: verify integrity hash inside parsed.meta
    Object.assign(NET_STATE, parsed.data || {});
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[Sync_Core] Corrupted state, starting fresh.", err);
  }
}

export async function saveState() {
  try {
    const data = {
      data: NET_STATE,
      meta: {
        version: NET_STATE.version,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    };
    const hash = await hashState(data);
    data.meta.hash = hash;

    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[Chatty Prime] Failed to persist state.", err);
  }
}
```

### `hash.js` (browser SubtleCrypto wrapper)

```js
// src/security/hash.js
export async function hashState(obj) {
  const json = JSON.stringify(obj);
  const enc = new TextEncoder().encode(json);

  if (!crypto || !crypto.subtle) {
    console.warn("[Chatty Prime] SubtleCrypto not available; hash skipped.");
    return "NO_HASH_ENV";
  }

  const buffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", enc);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
  return Array.from(bytes)
    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join("");
}
```

### `sanitize.js`

```js
// src/security/sanitize.js
export function sanitizeInput(input) {
  const temp = document.createElement("div");
  temp.textContent = input ?? "";
  return temp.textContent; // stays as plain text
}
```

## 6. UI Components

### `GlassCard.js`

```js
// src/ui/components/GlassCard.js
export function GlassCard({ title, body, footer } = {}) {
  const card = document.createElement("section");
  card.className = "glass-card";

  if (title) {
    const h = document.createElement("h2");
    h.className = "glass-card__title";
    h.textContent = title;
    card.appendChild(h);
  }

  if (body) {
    const div = document.createElement("div");
    div.className = "glass-card__body";
    if (typeof body === "string") {
      div.textContent = body;
    } else {
      div.appendChild(body);
    }
    card.appendChild(div);
  }

  if (footer) {
    const f = document.createElement("div");
    f.className = "glass-card__footer";
    if (typeof footer === "string") {
      f.textContent = footer;
    } else {
      f.appendChild(footer);
    }
    card.appendChild(f);
  }

  return card;
}
```

### `BuddyRow.js`

```js
// src/ui/components/BuddyRow.js
export function BuddyRow(buddy, { onSelect } = {}) {
  const row = document.createElement("button");
  row.className = "buddy-row";
  row.type = "button";
  row.dataset.id = buddy.id;

  const name = document.createElement("span");
  name.className = "buddy-row__name";
  name.textContent = buddy.name;

  const level = document.createElement("span");
  level.className = "buddy-row__level";
  level.textContent = buddy.level;

  row.appendChild(name);
  row.appendChild(level);

  if (onSelect) {
    row.addEventListener("click", () => onSelect(buddy));
  }

  return row;
}
```

## 7. Views (Dashboard + Buddy as examples)

### `DashboardView.js`

```js
// src/ui/views/DashboardView.js
import { NET_STATE } from "../../core/state.js";
import { GlassCard } from "../components/GlassCard.js";

export function DashboardView() {
  const root = document.createElement("div");
  root.className = "view view--dashboard";

  const hero = GlassCard({
    title: "Welcome to NET",
    body: "Learn English the natural way: listen, mimic, speak, repeat. Be a tiger, not a mouse.",
  });

  const metrics = GlassCard({
    title: "Today’s 1-Hour Lifestyle",
    body: renderMetrics(),
  });

  root.appendChild(hero);
  root.appendChild(metrics);
  return root;
}

function renderMetrics() {
  const wrap = document.createElement("div");
  wrap.className = "metrics";

  const l = document.createElement("p");
  l.textContent = `Listening: ${NET_STATE.metrics.dailyListeningMinutes}/${45} minutes`;

  const s = document.createElement("p");
  s.textContent = `Speaking: ${NET_STATE.metrics.dailySpeakingMinutes}/${15} minutes`;

  const c = document.createElement("p");
  c.textContent = `Confidence score: ${(NET_STATE.metrics.confidenceScore * 100).toFixed(0)}%`;

  wrap.appendChild(l);
  wrap.appendChild(s);
  wrap.appendChild(c);
  return wrap;
}
```

### `BuddyView.js`

```js
// src/ui/views/BuddyView.js
import { NET_STATE } from "../../core/state.js";
import { GlassCard } from "../components/GlassCard.js";
import { BuddyRow } from "../components/BuddyRow.js";

export function BuddyView() {
  const root = document.createElement("div");
  root.className = "view view--buddy";

  const card = GlassCard({
    title: "Buddy System",
  });

  const list = document.createElement("div");
  list.className = "buddy-list";

  NET_STATE.buddies.forEach((b) => {
    const row = BuddyRow(b, {
      onSelect: (buddy) => {
        NET_STATE.selectedStudentId = buddy.id;
        // In a fuller version, you'd trigger a re-render / message
      },
    });
    list.appendChild(row);
  });

  card.querySelector(".glass-card__body")?.appendChild(list);
  root.appendChild(card);
  return root;
}
```

## 8. Router & App Bootstrap

### `router.js`

```js
// src/router.js
import { NET_STATE } from "./core/state.js";
import { DashboardView } from "./ui/views/DashboardView.js";
import { BuddyView } from "./ui/views/BuddyView.js";
// TODO: import LessonsView, SessionView, UniverseView

export function renderActiveView(rootEl) {
  rootEl.innerHTML = "";

  let viewNode;
  switch (NET_STATE.activeView) {
    case "buddy":
      viewNode = BuddyView();
      break;
    case "dashboard":
    default:
      viewNode = DashboardView();
  }

  rootEl.appendChild(viewNode);
}
```

### `app.js`

```js
// src/app.js
import { loadState, saveState } from "./core/storage.js";
import { NET_STATE } from "./core/state.js";
import { renderActiveView } from "./router.js";

function setupNav(rootEl) {
  const nav = document.querySelector("[data-net-nav]");
  if (!nav) return;

  nav.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    const btn = e.target.closest("[data-view]");
    if (!btn) return;

    const view = btn.dataset.view;
    if (!view) return;

    NET_STATE.activeView = view;
    renderActiveView(rootEl);
    saveState();
  });
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
  const rootEl = document.querySelector("#app");
  if (!rootEl) {
    console.error("No #app root found");
    return;
  }

  loadState();
  renderActiveView(rootEl);
  setupNav(rootEl);
});
```

```css
:root {
  --color-purple: #a855f7;
  --color-gold: #f59e0b;
  --color-dark: #0f0f1a;

  --glass-bg: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.8);
  --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
}

*,
*::before,
*::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

body {
  margin: 0;
  min-height: 100vh;
  font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Inter", sans-serif;
  color: #e5e7eb;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3), transparent),
    radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.25), transparent),
    var(--color-dark);
}

.app-shell {
  max-width: 1120px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.app-header {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.app-header h1 {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}

.app-nav {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.app-nav button {
  padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;
  border-radius: 999px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5);
  background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.9);
  color: #e5e7eb;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
}

.app-nav button:hover {
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.7);
}

.app-main {
  display: grid;
  gap: 1.5rem;
}

/* GlassCard */

.glass-card {
  background: var(--glass-bg);
  border-radius: 1.5rem;
  border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
  padding: 1.25rem 1.5rem;
  backdrop-filter: blur(12px) saturate(150%);
  box-shadow:
    0 0 18px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4),
    0 0 6px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
}

.glass-card__title {
  font-size: 1.2rem;
  margin: 0 0 0.75rem;
}

.glass-card__body {
  font-size: 0.95rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.buddy-list {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  margin-top: 0.75rem;
}

.buddy-row {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  border-radius: 0.9rem;
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 27, 221, 0.6);
  padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
  background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.8);
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}

.buddy-row__name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.buddy-row__level {
  opacity: 0.75;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
}
```
