```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8" />
  <title>NET App V0 — Buddy System First</title>
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
  <style>
    :root {
      --color-bg: #0f0f1a;
      --color-card-bg: rgba(26, 26, 38, 0.8);
      --color-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
      --color-purple: #a855f7;
      --color-gold: #f59e0b;
      --color-text: #e5e7eb;
      --shadow-soft: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6), 0 0 8px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35);
      --radius-lg: 1.5rem;
      --transition-fast: 0.2s cubic-bezier(0.68,-0.55,0.265,1.55);
      --glass-blur: 12px; /* Gospel 444 minimum */
    }

    * {
      box-sizing: border-box;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
    }

    html, body {
      height: 100%;
    }

    body {
      font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Inter", sans-serif;
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 10% 20%, rgba(168,85,247,0.25) 0, transparent 55%),
                  radial-gradient(circle at 90% 80%, rgba(245,158,11,0.22) 0, transparent 60%),
                  var(--color-bg);
      color: var(--color-text);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: center;
      align-items: stretch;
      padding: 1.5rem;
    }

    .app-shell {
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 1200px;
      min-height: 90vh;
      border-radius: 1.75rem;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(15,15,26,0.95), rgba(15,15,26,0.7));
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.15);
      box-shadow: 0 0 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
      display: flex;
      overflow: hidden;
      position: relative;
    }

    /* Ambient blobs (background depth, no blue) */
    .blob {
      position: absolute;
      width: 260px;
      height: 260px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      filter: blur(80px);
      opacity: 0.55;
      pointer-events: none;
      mix-blend-mode: screen;
      animation: blob-move 18s infinite alternate;
    }
    .blob--left {
      left: -60px;
      top: -40px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(168,85,247,0.9), transparent 70%);
    }
    .blob--right {
      right: -80px;
      bottom: -40px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(245,158,11,0.9), transparent 70%);
      animation-delay: 4s;
    }
    @keyframes blob-move {
      0% { transform: translate3d(0,0,0) scale(1); }
      100% { transform: translate3d(40px,-30px,0) scale(1.08); }
    }

    /* Sidebar */
    .sidebar {
      width: 240px;
      backdrop-filter: blur(var(--glass-blur)) saturate(150%);
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,15,26,0.95), rgba(15,15,26,0.75));
      border-right: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.2);
      padding: 1.5rem 1.4rem;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 1.75rem;
      position: relative;
      z-index: 2;
    }

    .logo-lockup {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.75rem;
    }

    .logo-orb {
      width: 38px;
      height: 38px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--color-purple), transparent 70%);
      box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(168,85,247,0.95);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      color: var(--color-text);
      font-weight: 700;
      letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    }

    .logo-text-main {
      font-size: 1.05rem;
      font-weight: 700;
      letter-spacing: 0.08em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
    }

    .logo-text-sub {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      opacity: 0.7;
    }

    .nav-section-label {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      letter-spacing: 0.12em;
      opacity: 0.6;
    }

    .nav-list {
      list-style: none;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    .nav-btn {
      width: 100%;
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 0.55rem 0.8rem;
      background: transparent;
      border: 1px solid transparent;
      color: rgba(226,232,240,0.8);
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      font-size: 0.9rem;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast),
                  box-shadow var(--transition-fast), border-color var(--transition-fast),
                  color var(--transition-fast);
    }

    .nav-btn-label {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    .nav-dot {
      width: 6px;
      height: 6px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(148,163,184,0.7);
    }

    .nav-tag {
      font-size: 0.65rem;
      padding: 0.1rem 0.55rem;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.5);
      opacity: 0.85;
    }

    .nav-btn:hover {
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.7);
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      border-color: rgba(148,163,184,0.5);
    }

    .nav-btn--active {
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 0 50%, rgba(168,85,247,0.35), transparent 65%);
      border-color: var(--color-purple);
      box-shadow: var(--shadow-soft);
      color: var(--color-text);
    }

    .nav-btn--active .nav-dot {
      background: var(--color-gold);
      box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(245,158,11,0.9);
    }

    .sidebar-footer {
      margin-top: auto;
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      opacity: 0.65;
    }

    .sidebar-footer-strong {
      color: var(--color-gold);
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    /* Main content */
    .main {
      flex: 1;
      position: relative;
      padding: 1.5rem 1.75rem;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 1.25rem;
      z-index: 2;
    }

    .top-row {
      display: flex;
      gap: 1rem;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: space-between;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .page-title-block {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 0.25rem;
    }

    .page-eyebrow {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      letter-spacing: 0.14em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
      color: rgba(148,163,184,0.85);
    }

    .page-title {
      font-size: 1.35rem;
      font-weight: 650;
      letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    }

    .page-title span {
      color: var(--color-purple);
    }

    .page-subtitle {
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      color: rgba(148,163,184,0.9);
    }

    .metric-chip-row {
      display: flex;
      gap: 0.6rem;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .metric-chip {
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 0.4rem 0.75rem;
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.8);
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.45);
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    .metric-dot {
      width: 8px;
      height: 8px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: var(--color-gold);
      box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(245,158,11,0.8);
    }

    /* Cards layout */
    .grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 1.1fr;
      gap: 1.2rem;
    }

    @media (max-width: 900px) {
      .app-shell {
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      .sidebar {
        width: 100%;
        flex-direction: row;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 1rem;
        overflow-x: auto;
      }
      .nav-section-label,
      .sidebar-footer {
        display: none;
      }
      .main {
        padding: 1.2rem;
      }
      .grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      }
    }

    .card {
      border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(15,23,42,0.98), rgba(15,23,42,0.78));
      border: 1px solid var(--color-border);
      box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(15,23,42,0.85);
      padding: 1.2rem 1.1rem;
      backdrop-filter: blur(var(--glass-blur)) saturate(155%);
      position: relative;
      overflow: hidden;
    }

    .card-header {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      margin-bottom: 0.85rem;
      gap: 0.75rem;
    }

    .card-title-block {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 0.15rem;
    }

    .card-title {
      font-size: 0.98rem;
      font-weight: 600;
      letter-spacing: 0.04em;
      text-transform: uppercase;
    }

    .card-kicker {
      font-size: 0.75rem;
      color: rgba(148,163,184,0.95);
    }

    .card-pill {
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      padding: 0.25rem 0.7rem;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(168,85,247,0.5);
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 0 50%, rgba(168,85,247,0.35), transparent 65%);
      color: var(--color-text);
      display: inline-flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.35rem;
    }

    .card-pill-dot {
      width: 6px;
      height: 6px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: var(--color-gold);
    }

    .card-body {
      font-size: 0.86rem;
      color: rgba(226,232,240,0.95);
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 0.7rem;
    }

    .tag-row {
      display: flex;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      gap: 0.4rem;
    }

    .tag {
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      padding: 0.2rem 0.6rem;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.55);
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.9);
    }

    .tag--success {
      border-color: rgba(34,197,94,0.7);
      color: #bbf7d0;
    }

    .tag--pending {
      border-color: rgba(245,158,11,0.7);
      color: #fed7aa;
    }

    /* Buddy list */
    .buddy-list {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 0.45rem;
      margin-top: 0.35rem;
    }

    .buddy-row {
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      padding: 0.4rem 0.55rem;
      border-radius: 0.9rem;
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.9);
      border: 1px solid rgba(30,64,175,0); /* no blue shadow, keep transparent */
      transition: background var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast), box-shadow var(--transition-fast);
      cursor: pointer;
    }

    .buddy-row:hover {
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 0 50%, rgba(168,85,247,0.25), transparent 70%);
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(168,85,247,0.55);
    }

    .buddy-main {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 0.6rem;
    }

    .buddy-avatar {
      width: 26px;
      height: 26px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(168,85,247,0.4), transparent 65%);
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.6);
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      font-weight: 600;
    }

    .buddy-name {
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      font-weight: 500;
    }

    .buddy-meta {
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      color: rgba(148,163,184,0.95);
    }

    .buddy-stat-pill {
      font-size: 0.7rem;
      padding: 0.18rem 0.5rem;
      border-radius: 999px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(245,158,11,0.7);
      background: rgba(24,16,6,0.9);
      color: #fed7aa;
    }

    /* Challenge Builder */
    .field-row {
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: 0.2rem;
      margin-top: 0.3rem;
    }

    .field-label {
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: rgba(148,163,184,0.95);
    }

    .field-input,
    .field-select,
    .field-textarea {
      width: 100%;
      border-radius: 0.85rem;
      border: 1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.6);
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.95);
      color: var(--color-text);
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      padding: 0.5rem 0.6rem;
      outline: none;
      transition: border-color var(--transition-fast), box-shadow var(--transition-fast), background var(--transition-fast);
    }

    .field-input:focus,
    .field-select:focus,
    .field-textarea:focus {
      border-color: var(--color-purple);
      box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(168,85,247,0.6);
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.98);
    }

    .field-textarea {
      min-height: 80px;
      resize: vertical;
    }

    .btn-row {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: flex-end;
      gap: 0.6rem;
      margin-top: 0.6rem;
    }

    .btn {
      border-radius: 999px;
      padding: 0.45rem 0.9rem;
      font-size: 0.8rem;
      border: 1px solid transparent;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: background var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast),
                  box-shadow var(--transition-fast), border-color var(--transition-fast);
    }

    .btn-secondary {
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.9);
      border-color: rgba(148,163,184,0.6);
      color: rgba(226,232,240,0.9);
    }

    .btn-secondary:hover {
      background: rgba(15,23,42,0.98);
      transform: translateY(-1px);
      box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(148,163,184,0.7);
    }

    .btn-primary {
      background: radial-gradient(circle at 0 50%, rgba(168,85,247,0.9), rgba(245,158,11,0.9));
      border-color: rgba(248,250,252,0.85);
      color: #0f0f1a;
      font-weight: 600;
      box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(168,85,247,0.9), 0 0 10px rgba(245,158,11,0.85);
    }

    .btn-primary:hover {
      transform: translateY(-1px) scale(1.01);
      box-shadow: 0 0 22px rgba(168,85,247,1), 0 0 14px rgba(245,158,11,0.95);
    }

    .status-line {
      font-size: 0.78rem;
      color: rgba(148,163,184,0.95);
      margin-top: 0.45rem;
    }

    .status-line span {
      color: var(--color-gold);
      font-weight: 500;
    }

    /* Utility hide */
    .hidden {
      display: none !important;
    }

  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="app-shell">
    <div class="blob blob--left"></div>
    <div class="blob blob--right"></div>

    <!-- Sidebar -->
    <aside class="sidebar">
      <div class="logo-lockup">
        <div class="logo-orb">NET</div>
        <div>
          <div class="logo-text-main">Natural English</div>
          <div class="logo-text-sub">Training • Saigon</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <div class="nav-section-label">Main</div>
        <ul class="nav-list">
          <li><button class="nav-btn nav-btn--active" data-view="dashboard">
            <span class="nav-btn-label">
              <span class="nav-dot"></span>
              <span>Dashboard</span>
            </span>
            <span class="nav-tag">Today</span>
          </button></li>
          <li><button class="nav-btn" data-view="lessons">
            <span class="nav-btn-label">
              <span class="nav-dot"></span>
              <span>Lessons</span>
            </span>
            <span class="nav-tag">Academies</span>
          </button></li>
          <li><button class="nav-btn" data-view="buddies">
            <span class="nav-btn-label">
              <span class="nav-dot"></span>
              <span>Buddy System</span>
            </span>
            <span class="nav-tag">V0 Focus</span>
          </button></li>
          <li><button class="nav-btn" data-view="community">
            <span class="nav-btn-label">
              <span class="nav-dot"></span>
              <span>Community</span>
            </span>
            <span class="nav-tag">Networking</span>
          </button></li>
          <li><button class="nav-btn" data-view="settings">
            <span class="nav-btn-label">
              <span class="nav-dot"></span>
              <span>Settings</span>
            </span>
            <span class="nav-tag">Profile</span>
          </button></li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div class="sidebar-footer">
        <div>Executor: <span class="sidebar-footer-strong">Michael Hobbs</span></div>
        <div style="margin-top:0.15rem;">Build: <span>V0 • Buddy First</span></div>
      </div>
    </aside>

    <!-- Main -->
    <main class="main">
      <div class="top-row">
        <div class="page-title-block">
          <div class="page-eyebrow" id="view-eyebrow">NET • Buddy-First Build</div>
          <div class="page-title" id="view-title">
            Welcome back, <span>Student</span>
          </div>
          <div class="page-subtitle" id="view-subtitle">
            1 hour a day, confidence for life. Buddy System is the core of V0.
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="metric-chip-row">
          <div class="metric-chip">
            <span class="metric-dot"></span>
            <span><strong>Streak:</strong> <span id="metric-streak">3 days</span></span>
          </div>
          <div class="metric-chip">
            <span><strong>Today:</strong> <span id="metric-today">30 / 60 min</span></span>
          </div>
          <div class="metric-chip">
            <span><strong>Buddy Session:</strong> <span id="metric-next-buddy">Tonight 8:30 PM</span></span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- DASHBOARD VIEW -->
      <section id="view-dashboard">
        <div class="grid">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Today’s 1-Hour Plan</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">45 min listening • 15 min speaking</div>
              </div>
              <div class="card-pill">
                <span class="card-pill-dot"></span>
                <span>Natural English • V0</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <div>
                <strong>Listening Block (45 min)</strong>
                <p style="margin-top:0.25rem;">Finish episode 2 of <em>The Office</em> (US) with English subtitles only. No pausing, just natural listening. Aim for 60%+ understanding — don’t chase every word.</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <strong>Speaking Block (15 min)</strong>
                <p style="margin-top:0.25rem;">Shadow 2–3 scenes from the same episode. Focus on speed, rhythm, and volume — no robot voice. Be a <strong>tiger</strong>, not a mouse.</p>
              </div>
              <div class="tag-row">
                <div class="tag">Pillar 1: Listen & Understand</div>
                <div class="tag">Pillar 2: Speak & Mimic</div>
                <div class="tag">1-Hour Lifestyle</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Quick Snapshot</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Confidence, volume & consistency</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <div class="tag-row">
                <div class="tag tag--success">Confidence: 7 / 10</div>
                <div class="tag tag--pending">Volume: 5 / 10</div>
                <div class="tag">Streak: 3 days</div>
              </div>
              <p style="margin-top:0.35rem;">Keep your streak alive. Your next big jump in confidence comes from speaking <strong>out loud</strong> with your buddy — not just listening.</p>
              <p style="margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.8rem; color:rgba(148,163,184,0.95);"><strong>Tip:</strong> Treat NET like gym for your English. Small sessions every day beat “marathon” weekends.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- LESSONS VIEW (Placeholder) -->
      <section id="view-lessons" class="hidden">
        <div class="grid">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Academies Overview</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Each academy = one story world</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <p><strong>Sarcasm Academy:</strong> Learn tone, humor, and sarcasm so you can actually understand jokes with foreigners.</p>
              <p><strong>Careers Academy:</strong> Meetings, emails, small talk, negotiations — English for real business situations.</p>
              <p><strong>Context Academy:</strong> Stop translating every word. Learn to read between the lines.</p>
              <p><strong>Pronunciation Academy:</strong> Master all 42 sounds, kill the “robot voice,” and fix your volume.</p>
              <div class="tag-row" style="margin-top:0.4rem;">
                <div class="tag">10 Vocab</div>
                <div class="tag">5 Idioms</div>
                <div class="tag">5 Slang</div>
                <div class="tag">3 Games</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Next Lesson</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Universe Era • V4 ready</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <p><strong>Coming next:</strong> full Lesson Player with rooms, games, team scoring, and StoryFlow branching.</p>
              <p style="margin-top:0.4rem; font-size:0.8rem; color:rgba(148,163,184,0.95);">For now, V0 focuses on your <strong>Buddy System</strong>. That’s where confidence is forged.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- BUDDY SYSTEM VIEW (V0 FOCUS) -->
      <section id="view-buddies" class="hidden">
        <div class="grid">
          <!-- Left: Buddy list & selection -->
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Buddy Roster</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Choose who you practice with this week</div>
              </div>
              <div class="card-pill">
                <span class="card-pill-dot"></span>
                <span>Confidence Engine</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <div class="tag-row">
                <div class="tag">Weekly Target: 1h</div>
                <div class="tag">Mode: Video / Call / Café</div>
                <div class="tag tag--success">Safe to make mistakes</div>
              </div>

              <div class="buddy-list" id="buddy-list">
                <!-- Filled by JS -->
              </div>

              <div class="status-line" id="buddy-status-line">
                Selected buddy: <span>None yet</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Right: Session builder -->
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Build This Week’s Buddy Session</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Make it small, fun, and repeatable</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <div class="field-row">
                <label class="field-label" for="session-goal">Session Goal</label>
                <select id="session-goal" class="field-select">
                  <option value="confidence">Confidence & Volume</option>
                  <option value="small-talk">Small Talk & Networking</option>
                  <option value="business">Business English (Meetings/Email)</option>
                  <option value="pronunciation">Pronunciation & Accent</option>
                  <option value="storytelling">Storytelling Practice</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div class="field-row">
                <label class="field-label" for="session-length">Length</label>
                <select id="session-length" class="field-select">
                  <option value="15">15 minutes (tiny win)</option>
                  <option value="30">30 minutes</option>
                  <option value="45">45 minutes</option>
                  <option value="60">60 minutes</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div class="field-row">
                <label class="field-label" for="session-topic">Topic Prompt</label>
                <select id="session-topic" class="field-select">
                  <option value="weekend">Weekend stories</option>
                  <option value="work">This week at work</option>
                  <option value="dreams">Dream job & future plans</option>
                  <option value="travel">Travel, cities, and cultures</option>
                  <option value="movies">Movies, series, and games</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div class="field-row">
                <label class="field-label" for="session-notes">Session Script / Notes</label>
                <textarea id="session-notes" class="field-textarea" placeholder="Example: 5 minutes warm-up small talk, 10 minutes taking turns telling a story, 5 minutes feedback on volume and confidence."></textarea>
              </div>

              <div class="btn-row">
                <button class="btn btn-secondary" id="btn-fill-template">Auto-fill Idea</button>
                <button class="btn btn-primary" id="btn-lock-session">Lock This Session</button>
              </div>

              <div class="status-line" id="session-status-line">
                No session locked yet. Build something small and consistent.
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- COMMUNITY VIEW -->
      <section id="view-community" class="hidden">
        <div class="grid">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Saigon Networking Nights</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Monthly, real-world English</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <p><strong>Coming soon in V1:</strong> event listing, RSVP, and automatic follow-up prompts so you can practice small talk with real people, not just screens.</p>
              <p style="margin-top:0.35rem;">Idea: “Bùi Viện but for English & careers” — tables for different topics: Tech, Business, Travel, Comedy, Startups.</p>
              <div class="tag-row" style="margin-top:0.4rem;">
                <div class="tag">Real Conversations</div>
                <div class="tag">Confidence Training</div>
                <div class="tag">Career Networking</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Community Roadmap</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">V0 → V4 universe</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <p><strong>V0:</strong> Buddy System core.</p>
              <p><strong>V1:</strong> Events + RSVP.</p>
              <p><strong>V2:</strong> In-app voice rooms, rotating topics.</p>
              <p><strong>V3–V4:</strong> Full “Living Classroom Universe” — characters, arcs, and quests across all academies.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <!-- SETTINGS VIEW -->
      <section id="view-settings" class="hidden">
        <div class="grid">
          <div class="card">
            <div class="card-header">
              <div class="card-title-block">
                <div class="card-title">Profile & Preferences</div>
                <div class="card-kicker">Tiny knobs, big difference</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
              <p>This is where you’ll eventually set:</p>
              <ul style="margin-left:1rem; margin-top:0.35rem; font-size:0.84rem; line-height:1.4;">
                <li>Preferred accent (US/UK)</li>
                <li>Content type (sitcoms, business, documentary, gaming)</li>
                <li>Study window (morning / evening)</li>
                <li>Buddy practice time slots</li>
              </ul>
              <p style="margin-top:0.4rem; font-size:0.8rem; color:rgba(148,163,184,0.95);">V0 keeps it simple. The most important setting right now is: <strong>show up daily</strong>.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  </div>

  <script>
    // --- Simple App State (front-end only, no storage yet) ---
    const state = {
      activeView: "dashboard",
      streakDays: 3,
      minutesToday: 30,
      buddies: [
        {
          id: "nhan",
          name: "Nhan",
          level: "B1 • Creative",
          focus: "Confidence & volume",
          sessionsThisWeek: 1,
          archetype: "Low-volume / shy"
        },
        {
          id: "kiet",
          name: "Kiet",
          level: "C1 • Teen Genius",
          focus: "Deep topics & debates",
          sessionsThisWeek: 0,
          archetype: "Advanced / fast-thinker"
        },
        {
          id: "quang",
          name: "Quang",
          level: "B1 • University",
          focus: "Presentations & phrasing",
          sessionsThisWeek: 0,
          archetype: "Corporate / student"
        }
      ],
      selectedBuddyId: null,
      lockedSession: null
    };

    // --- DOM references ---
    const navButtons = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".nav-btn"));
    const eyebrowEl = document.getElementById("view-eyebrow");
    const titleEl = document.getElementById("view-title");
    const subtitleEl = document.getElementById("view-subtitle");
    const metricStreakEl = document.getElementById("metric-streak");
    const metricTodayEl = document.getElementById("metric-today");
    const metricNextBuddyEl = document.getElementById("metric-next-buddy");
    const views = {
      dashboard: document.getElementById("view-dashboard"),
      lessons: document.getElementById("view-lessons"),
      buddies: document.getElementById("view-buddies"),
      community: document.getElementById("view-community"),
      settings: document.getElementById("view-settings")
    };
    const buddyListEl = document.getElementById("buddy-list");
    const buddyStatusLine = document.getElementById("buddy-status-line");
    const sessionGoalEl = document.getElementById("session-goal");
    const sessionLengthEl = document.getElementById("session-length");
    const sessionTopicEl = document.getElementById("session-topic");
    const sessionNotesEl = document.getElementById("session-notes");
    const sessionStatusLine = document.getElementById("session-status-line");
    const btnFillTemplate = document.getElementById("btn-fill-template");
    const btnLockSession = document.getElementById("btn-lock-session");

    // --- View meta text ---
    const viewMeta = {
      dashboard: {
        eyebrow: "NET • Buddy-First Build",
        title: "Welcome back, Student",
        subtitle: "1 hour a day, confidence for life. Buddy System is the core of V0."
      },
      lessons: {
        eyebrow: "NET • Academies Overview",
        title: "Your Lesson Worlds",
        subtitle: "Sarcasm, Careers, Context, Pronunciation — all waiting for StoryFlow."
      },
      buddies: {
        eyebrow: "NET • Buddy System Core",
        title: "Practice with Real People",
        subtitle: "You don’t beat fear alone. You beat it laughing with a partner."
      },
      community: {
        eyebrow: "NET • Community Universe",
        title: "Networking Nights & Events",
        subtitle: "From Zoom calls to real tables in Saigon. English has to leave the classroom."
      },
      settings: {
        eyebrow: "NET • Personal Settings",
        title: "Tune Your Experience",
        subtitle: "The right content at the right time makes the habit stick."
      }
    };

    function setActiveView(view) {
      state.activeView = view;

      // Update nav
      navButtons.forEach(btn => {
        if (btn.dataset.view === view) {
          btn.classList.add("nav-btn--active");
        } else {
          btn.classList.remove("nav-btn--active");
        }
      });

      // Show/Hide sections
      Object.entries(views).forEach(([key, section]) => {
        if (key === view) {
          section.classList.remove("hidden");
        } else {
          section.classList.add("hidden");
        }
      });

      // Update header text
      const meta = viewMeta[view];
      if (meta) {
        eyebrowEl.textContent = meta.eyebrow;
        titleEl.innerHTML = ""; // rebuild carefully to allow span color
        const text = meta.title;
        const spanIndex = text.indexOf("Student");
        if (view === "dashboard" && spanIndex !== -1) {
          // simple span injection: static, not user content
          const before = text.slice(0, spanIndex);
          const after = text.slice(spanIndex + "Student".length);
          const span = document.createElement("span");
          span.textContent = "Student";
          titleEl.append(before, span, after);
        } else {
          titleEl.textContent = text;
        }
        subtitleEl.textContent = meta.subtitle;
      }
    }

    function renderMetrics() {
      metricStreakEl.textContent = state.streakDays + " days";
      metricTodayEl.textContent = state.minutesToday + " / 60 min";
      // In V0 we keep buddy metric static; can link to real schedule later
      metricNextBuddyEl.textContent = "This week";
    }

    function renderBuddyList() {
      buddyListEl.innerHTML = ""; // static template, no user data HTML injection

      state.buddies.forEach(buddy => {
        const row = document.createElement("div");
        row.className = "buddy-row";
        row.dataset.buddyId = buddy.id;

        const main = document.createElement("div");
        main.className = "buddy-main";

        const avatar = document.createElement("div");
        avatar.className = "buddy-avatar";
        avatar.textContent = buddy.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase();

        const info = document.createElement("div");
        const nameEl = document.createElement("div");
        nameEl.className = "buddy-name";
        nameEl.textContent = buddy.name;
        const metaEl = document.createElement("div");
        metaEl.className = "buddy-meta";
        metaEl.textContent = buddy.level + " • " + buddy.focus;

        info.appendChild(nameEl);
        info.appendChild(metaEl);

        main.appendChild(avatar);
        main.appendChild(info);

        const statPill = document.createElement("div");
        statPill.className = "buddy-stat-pill";
        statPill.textContent = buddy.sessionsThisWeek + "x this week";

        row.appendChild(main);
        row.appendChild(statPill);

        row.addEventListener("click", () => {
          state.selectedBuddyId = buddy.id;
          updateBuddyStatus();
          highlightSelectedBuddy();
        });

        buddyListEl.appendChild(row);
      });

      updateBuddyStatus();
      highlightSelectedBuddy();
    }

    function updateBuddyStatus() {
      const span = buddyStatusLine.querySelector("span");
      if (!span) return;
      if (!state.selectedBuddyId) {
        span.textContent = "None yet";
      } else {
        const buddy = state.buddies.find(b => b.id === state.selectedBuddyId);
        if (buddy) {
          span.textContent = buddy.name + " (" + buddy.level + ")";
        }
      }
    }

    function highlightSelectedBuddy() {
      const rows = buddyListEl.querySelectorAll(".buddy-row");
      rows.forEach(row => {
        const id = row.dataset.buddyId;
        if (id === state.selectedBuddyId) {
          row.style.boxShadow = "0 0 18px rgba(245,158,11,0.85)";
          row.style.borderColor = "rgba(245,158,11,0.9)";
        } else {
          row.style.boxShadow = "none";
          row.style.borderColor = "rgba(30,64,175,0)";
        }
      });
    }

    function autoFillSessionIdea() {
      // This uses only static templates; no user injection risk
      const goal = sessionGoalEl.value;
      const length = Number(sessionLengthEl.value);
      const topic = sessionTopicEl.value;

      let template = "";

      const warmup = "5 minutes: warm-up small talk about your day.\n";
      const review = "5 minutes: quick review — each person shares 1 thing they did well and 1 thing to improve.\n";

      if (goal === "confidence") {
        template += warmup;
        template += (length - 10) + " minutes: take turns telling a story about the topic. The listener must ask follow-up questions.\n";
        template += review;
      } else if (goal === "small-talk") {
        template += warmup;
        template += (length - 10) + " minutes: imagine you just met at a networking event. Practice natural questions and reactions.\n";
        template += review;
      } else if (goal === "business") {
        template += warmup;
        template += (length - 10) + " minutes: role-play a short meeting or email discussion about work tasks.\n";
        template += review;
      } else if (goal === "pronunciation") {
        template += warmup;
        template += (length - 10) + " minutes: choose 5–8 sentences and drill volume, rhythm, and key sounds.\n";
        template += review;
      } else if (goal === "storytelling") {
        template += warmup;
        template += (length - 10) + " minutes: tell one strong story each. Focus on past tense, feelings, and details.\n";
        template += review;
      }

      if (topic === "weekend") {
        template += "\nTopic guide: what you did last weekend, funny moments, anything that went wrong.";
      } else if (topic === "work") {
        template += "\nTopic guide: this week at work — wins, problems, one conversation with your boss.";
      } else if (topic === "dreams") {
        template += "\nTopic guide: your dream job, country, or project in the next 5–10 years.";
      } else if (topic === "travel") {
        template += "\nTopic guide: best trip so far, where you want to go next, culture shocks.";
      } else if (topic === "movies") {
        template += "\nTopic guide: your favorite series or movie, why you like it, characters you relate to.";
      }

      sessionNotesEl.value = template;
      sessionStatusLine.textContent = "Draft generated. Edit anything you want, then lock this session.";
    }

    function lockSession() {
      if (!state.selectedBuddyId) {
        sessionStatusLine.textContent = "Choose a buddy first. This session is for two people, not a textbook.";
        return;
      }
      const buddy = state.buddies.find(b => b.id === state.selectedBuddyId);
      const plan = sessionNotesEl.value.trim();
      if (!plan) {
        sessionStatusLine.textContent = "Write or auto-fill a simple plan. Keep it small and realistic.";
        return;
      }

      state.lockedSession = {
        buddyId: buddy.id,
        goal: sessionGoalEl.value,
        length: Number(sessionLengthEl.value),
        topic: sessionTopicEl.value,
        notes: plan
      };

      sessionStatusLine.textContent =
        "Session locked with " + buddy.name +
        " for " + state.lockedSession.length +
        " minutes. The only job now: show up.";

      // In future, this would also:
      // - Save to backend / localStorage
      // - Sync with NET PET
      // - Trigger reminders
    }

    // --- Event wiring ---
    navButtons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const view = btn.dataset.view;
        if (!view) return;
        setActiveView(view);
      });
    });

    btnFillTemplate.addEventListener("click", autoFillSessionIdea);
    btnLockSession.addEventListener("click", lockSession);

    // --- Initial render ---
    renderMetrics();
    renderBuddyList();
    setActiveView("dashboard");
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

# 🟣 **2. THE NET PHILOSOPHY (FIRST PERSON)**

### *Your voice. Marketing + teaching identity.*

I created NET because the traditional English system in Vietnam is broken.

Vietnamese adults were never taught real English—they were taught *grammar*, *textbooks*, *tests*, and *fear*.
The entire country learned English backwards.

And the results are painfully obvious:

* They can read
* They can do grammar exercises
* But they freeze in real conversation
* They’re terrified of making mistakes
* They speak with low confidence
* They rely on translation
* They don’t know how to “think in English”

**NET exists to fix this.**

I teach natural English the way humans actually acquire language:

* **Listen first**
* **Mimic second**
* **Speak naturally**
* **Grammar later**
* **Reading/Writing last**

It’s how Vietnamese learned Vietnamese.
It’s how babies learn globally.
It’s how the brain is wired.

This is the **NET foundation**.

---

# 🟣 **3. THE NET METHOD (FIRST PERSON)**

## **3.1 The Four Pillars (Marketing + Teaching)**

Here is how I explain it to students:

### **1. Listen & Understand (45 minutes/day)**

Real comprehensible input
Sitcoms, YouTube, podcasts
*Understand at least 60%*
Never pause
English subtitles only

### **2. Speak & Mimic (15 minutes/day)**

Shadowing
Pronunciation
Volume training
Master the 42 Jolly Phonics sounds

### **3. Context Learning**

Never memorize isolated words
Always learn in full sentences
Use spaced repetition:
0 → 1 → 3 → 7 days

### **4. Confidence First**

Vietnamese fear mistakes
I break the “prison of the mind”
Through tough love, humor, and safe practice

---

# 🟣 **4. THE ELEPHANT STORY (FIRST PERSON)**

> “Vietnamese adults are like the elephant at the zoo.
> When it’s young, they chain it to a steel pole.
> It tries to escape for days… weeks… and eventually gives up.
> Later, the zookeepers replace the steel pole with a tiny bamboo stick.
> The elephant *could* break free anytime — but it doesn’t.
> Because its mind is still trapped.
>
> Vietnamese learners aren’t bad at English.
> They’re just stuck in a mental cage built by school, grammar, and fear.
> NET exists to break that cage.”

The Echo Chamber Arc

* Student target: Nhân (low confidence)
* Two paths:

  * Path A → Passive → Anxiety → Noise
  * Path B → Active → Flow → Victory

StoryFlow integrates emotional, psychological, and linguistic progress.

The NET Ecosystem is:

I help Vietnamese adults break the fear of mistakes, think in English, speak confidently, and build an English lifestyle that lasts forever.

**System Voice Summary**
This canon is the foundation upon which all future systems, agents, lessons, sales, and marketing are built.

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>NET — The Living Classroom | V5 Final</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444 AESTHETICS SYSTEM (V5 ULTRA)
           © Michael Hobbs — Executor
           NO BLUE ALLOWED.
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --neon-purple: #a855f7;
            --gold-accent: #f59e0b;
            --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --panel-bg: rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.6); /* More transparency for V5 */
            --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.25);
            --text-primary: #ffffff;
            --text-secondary: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
            --glow-strong: 0 0 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            --glow-gold: 0 0 25px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
            --radius-xl: 32px;
            --radius-lg: 24px;
            --radius-md: 16px;
            --font-ui: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            --font-code: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            --danger: #ef4444;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; scroll-behavior: smooth; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--deep-dark);
            /* V5: Deeper, richer background with multiple radial sources */
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 15% 20%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.12) 0%, transparent 50%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 85% 80%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.08) 0%, transparent 50%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.8) 0%, var(--deep-dark) 100%);
            font-family: var(--font-ui);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            height: 100vh;
            overflow: hidden;
            display: flex;
        }

        /* --- ANIMATIONS --- */
        @keyframes float { 0% { transform: translateY(0px); } 50% { transform: translateY(-10px); } 100% { transform: translateY(0px); } }
        @keyframes pulse-glow { 0% { box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4); } 50% { box-shadow: 0 0 35px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6); } 100% { box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4); } }
        @keyframes fadeInSlide { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- LAYOUT: SIDEBAR + MAIN --- */
        .app-shell { display: flex; width: 100%; height: 100%; }

        /* Sidebar (V5 Enhanced) */
        .sidebar {
            width: 280px;
            background: rgba(10, 10, 18, 0.85);
            border-right: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            padding: 40px 25px;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: space-between;
            backdrop-filter: blur(24px);
            z-index: 100;
            transition: width 0.3s ease;
        }

        .brand-lockup { margin-bottom: 50px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; }
        .brand-logo { 
            font-size: 2rem; 
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff 0%, var(--neon-purple) 100%);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            font-weight: 900;
            letter-spacing: -1px;
            font-family: var(--font-code);
        }
        .brand-tag {
            font-size: 0.65rem;
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);
            color: var(--neon-purple);
            padding: 4px 8px;
            border-radius: 100px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            font-weight: 700;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
        }

        /* Navigation (V5 Interactive) */
        .nav-menu { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
        .nav-item {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            padding: 14px 18px;
            border-radius: 14px;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            text-decoration: none;
            font-weight: 500;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
            border: 1px solid transparent;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        .nav-item:hover {
            background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            transform: translateX(5px);
        }
        .nav-item.active {
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2) 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.05) 100%);
            border-color: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15);
        }
        .nav-item.active::before {
            content: ''; position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 4px; background: var(--neon-purple);
        }
        .nav-icon { margin-right: 14px; font-size: 1.2rem; opacity: 0.8; }
        .nav-item.active .nav-icon { opacity: 1; filter: drop-shadow(0 0 5px var(--neon-purple)); }

        /* User Profile Mini (V5) */
        .user-mini {
            padding: 20px;
            background: linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.05), rgba(255,255,255,0.02));
            border-radius: 18px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 12px;
            transition: all 0.2s ease;
        }
        .user-mini:hover { border-color: var(--gold-accent); box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2); }
        .user-avatar {
            width: 40px; height: 40px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: var(--gold-accent);
            display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
            font-weight: 700; color: var(--deep-dark);
            font-family: var(--font-code);
        }
        .user-info h4 { font-size: 0.95rem; margin-bottom: 2px; }
        .user-info p { font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-secondary); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }

        /* --- MAIN CONTENT AREA --- */
        .main-content {
            flex: 1;
            overflow-y: auto;
            overflow-x: hidden;
            padding: 0; /* Full bleed for hero */
            position: relative;
        }

        /* --- V5 HERO SECTION (Immersive) --- */
        .hero-section {
            min-height: 450px;
            padding: 60px 50px;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,15,26,0) 0%, var(--deep-dark) 100%),
                        url('data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciIHdpZHRoPSIxMDAiIGhlaWdodD0iMTAwIj48cmVjdCB3aWR0aD0iMTAwJSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxMDAlIiBmaWxsPSIjMGYwZjFhIi8+PGNpcmNsZSBjeD0iNTAiIGN5PSI1MCIgcj0iMSIgZmlsbD0icmdiYSgyNTUsMjU1LDI1NSwwLjEpIi8+PC9zdmc+'); /* Subtle starfield pattern */
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
            position: relative;
        }
        
        .hero-eyebrow {
            font-family: var(--font-code);
            color: var(--gold-accent);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 10px;
            animation: fadeInSlide 0.8s ease;
        }
        .hero-eyebrow::before { content: ''; width: 30px; height: 2px; background: var(--gold-accent); }

        .hero-title {
            font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 5vw, 4rem);
            font-weight: 800;
            line-height: 1.1;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
            max-width: 800px;
            animation: fadeInSlide 0.8s ease 0.1s backwards;
        }
        .hero-title span { 
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, #fff, var(--neon-purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        .hero-desc {
            font-size: 1.1rem;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            max-width: 600px;
            line-height: 1.6;
            margin-bottom: 35px;
            animation: fadeInSlide 0.8s ease 0.2s backwards;
        }

        .hero-actions { display: flex; gap: 15px; animation: fadeInSlide 0.8s ease 0.3s backwards; }

        /* --- V5 CONTENT CONTAINER --- */
        .content-wrapper {
            padding: 0 50px 60px;
            max-width: 1400px;
            margin: 0 auto;
        }

        /* --- CARDS & GRIDS (V5) --- */
        .grid-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 30px; }
        .grid-3 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr); gap: 30px; }

        .glass-card {
            background: var(--panel-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
            padding: 30px;
            transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.8, 0.25, 1);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            animation: fadeInSlide 0.6s ease backwards;
        }
        .glass-card::before {
            content: ''; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 4px;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--neon-purple), transparent);
            opacity: 0.5; transition: opacity 0.3s;
        }
        .glass-card:hover {
            transform: translateY(-5px);
            box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
            border-color: var(--neon-purple);
        }
        .glass-card:hover::before { opacity: 1; }

        .card-header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        .card-title { font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-primary); }
        .card-tag { 
            font-size: 0.75rem; padding: 5px 12px; 
            background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1); color: var(--gold-accent); 
            border-radius: 100px; border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
            font-family: var(--font-code);
        }

        /* --- BUDDY SYSTEM UI (V5 INTEGRATED) --- */
        .buddy-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
        .buddy-row {
            display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;
            padding: 16px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03); border-radius: 16px;
            border: 1px solid transparent; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.2s;
        }
        .buddy-row:hover, .buddy-row.selected {
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1); border-color: var(--neon-purple);
            transform: translateX(5px);
        }
        .buddy-avatar {
            width: 40px; height: 40px; border-radius: 50%;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(168,85,247,0.4), rgba(245,158,11,0.1));
            display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
            font-weight: 700; color: #fff; font-size: 1rem;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
        }

        /* Form Elements (V5) */
        .form-group { margin-bottom: 20px; }
        .form-label { 
            display: block; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-secondary); 
            margin-bottom: 8px; font-weight: 500; letter-spacing: 0.5px;
        }
        .form-input, select, textarea {
            width: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15);
            color: white; padding: 14px 16px; border-radius: 14px;
            font-family: var(--font-ui); font-size: 0.95rem;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }
        .form-input:focus, select:focus, textarea:focus {
            outline: none; border-color: var(--neon-purple);
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.05);
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
        }

        /* Buttons (V5) */
        .btn {
            padding: 14px 28px; border-radius: 100px; font-weight: 600;
            cursor: pointer; border: none; font-size: 1rem;
            transition: all 0.3s; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
        }
        .btn-primary {
            background: var(--neon-purple); color: white;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            position: relative; overflow: hidden;
        }
        .btn-primary:hover { 
            transform: translateY(-3px); 
            box-shadow: 0 8px 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5); 
        }
        .btn-secondary {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.08); color: white;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
        }
        .btn-secondary:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.15); transform: translateY(-2px); }

        /* Toast */
        .toast {
            position: fixed; bottom: 40px; right: 40px;
            background: rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.95); border: 1px solid var(--gold-accent);
            padding: 18px 28px; border-radius: 16px;
            box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
            transform: translateY(150px); opacity: 0;
            transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.68, -0.55, 0.265, 1.55);
            z-index: 1000; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
        }
        .toast.show { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 1; }

        /* --- RESPONSIVE --- */
        @media (max-width: 1024px) {
            .app-shell { flex-direction: column; }
            .sidebar { width: 100%; height: auto; flex-direction: row; align-items: center; padding: 20px; }
            .nav-menu { flex-direction: row; overflow-x: auto; }
            .nav-item span { display: none; }
            .nav-icon { margin: 0; font-size: 1.4rem; }
            .user-mini, .brand-tag { display: none; }
            .main-content { height: calc(100vh - 80px); }
            .grid-2, .grid-3 { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
            .hero-title { font-size: 2.5rem; }
            .content-wrapper { padding: 0 20px 40px; }
        }

        /* Hide views */
        .view-section { display: none; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.3s; }
        .view-section.active { display: block; opacity: 1; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>

    <div class="app-shell">
        <!-- SIDEBAR -->
        <nav class="sidebar">
            <div>
                <div class="brand-lockup">
                    <div class="brand-logo">NET</div>

                        <span>Dashboard</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="nav-item" data-target="buddies">

                        <span>Community</span>
                    </div>

            </div>

            <div class="user-mini">
                <div class="user-avatar">SA</div>

                    <p>B1 • Intermediate</p>

            </div>
        </nav>

        <!-- MAIN CONTENT -->
        <main class="main-content">
            
            <!-- VIEW: DASHBOARD -->
            <div id="dashboard" class="view-section active">
                <div class="hero-section">
                    <div class="hero-eyebrow">Natural English Training</div>
                    <h1 class="hero-title">Break the <br><span>Grammar Prison.</span></h1>
                    <p class="hero-desc">
                        You didn't learn grammar before you learned to speak. Why start now? 
                        NET is not a class. It's a lifestyle. Listen. Mimic. Speak.
                    </p>
                    <div class="hero-actions">
                        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchView('buddies')">Start Your Session</button>
                        <button class="btn btn-secondary">Watch Intro</button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="content-wrapper">
                    <div class="grid-2">
                        <!-- Today's Mission Card -->
                        <div class="glass-card">
                            <div class="card-header">
                                <div class="card-title">Today's Mission</div>
                                <div class="card-tag">Daily Goal</div>
                            </div>
                            <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
                                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; margin-bottom:8px; color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.9rem;">
                                    <span>Listening (45m)</span>
                                    <span>The Office US (S2E3)</span>
                                </div>
                                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; color:var(--text-secondary); font-size:0.9rem;">
                                    <span>Speaking (15m)</span>
                                    <span>Shadowing: Volume Focus</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1); height: 8px; border-radius: 100px; overflow: hidden;">
                                <div style="width: 60%; height: 100%; background: var(--neon-purple); box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--neon-purple);"></div>
                            </div>
                            <div style="text-align:right; font-size:0.8rem; margin-top:8px; color:var(--neon-purple); font-weight:600;">60% Complete</div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Tiger Voice Card -->
                        <div class="glass-card" style="border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);">
                            <div class="card-header">
                                <div class="card-title">Tiger Voice Check</div>
                                <div class="card-tag">Mindset</div>
                            </div>
                            <p style="font-size: 1rem; color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.6;">
                                "Vietnamese students are like elephants chained to a bamboo stick. You are strong enough to break free. Today, speak <strong>20% louder</strong> than you feel comfortable."
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- VIEW: BUDDIES -->
            <div id="buddies" class="view-section">
                <div class="hero-section" style="min-height: 300px; padding-bottom: 20px;">
                    <h1 class="hero-title">Find Your Partner.</h1>
                    <p class="hero-desc">Confidence is built in conversation, not in textbooks.</p>
                </div>
                
                <div class="content-wrapper">

                            <div class="card-header">
                                <div class="card-title">Available Partners</div>

                            <div class="form-group">

                                    <option value="storytelling">📖 Storytelling</option>

            <!-- VIEW: ACADEMIES -->

                        </div>

                            <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="width: 100%;">Enter Lobby</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- VIEW: COMMUNITY -->
