                <div class="hero-section" style="min-height: 300px; padding-bottom: 20px;">
                    <h1 class="hero-title">Real World. Real People.</h1>
                    <p class="hero-desc">Networking nights in Saigon. Where practice meets reality.</p>
                </div>

                <div class="content-wrapper">
                    <div class="glass-card" style="border: 1px solid var(--gold-accent); background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.05);">
                        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 20px;">
                            <div>
                                <h3 style="font-size: 1.5rem; font-weight: 700; color: #fff;">📅 Next Saigon Networking Night</h3>
                                <p style="color: var(--gold-accent); font-weight: 600; margin: 10px 0;">Friday, Nov 24th • 7:00 PM • District 1</p>
                                <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); max-width: 500px;">Real world practice. No teachers. No grades. Just professionals talking business and life. Mandatory for Level 3+.</p>
                            </div>
                            <button class="btn btn-primary" style="background: var(--gold-accent); color: var(--deep-dark);">RSVP Now</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- TOAST NOTIFICATION -->
    <div id="toast" class="toast">
        <span style="font-size: 2rem;">🐯</span>
        <div>
            <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--gold-accent); font-size: 1.1rem;">Session Locked</div>
            <div style="font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-secondary);">Get ready to speak up!</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           PHOENIX ENGINE LITE (V0.9)
           Logic: State, Persistence, Interaction, Security
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

        // State Management
        const defaultState = {
            streak: 3,
            activeView: 'dashboard',
            selectedBuddy: null,
            sessions: []
        };

        let state = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('netAppV5')) || defaultState;

        // Mock Data (Simulating DB)
        const buddies = [
            { id: 1, name: 'Nhan', level: 'B1', focus: 'Confidence' },
            { id: 2, name: 'Kiet', level: 'C1', focus: 'Tech/Gaming' },
            { id: 3, name: 'Quang', level: 'B2', focus: 'Presentation' }
        ];

        // --- NAVIGATION LOGIC ---
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
                item.classList.add('active');
                switchView(item.dataset.target);
            });
        });

        function switchView(viewId) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.view-section').forEach(v => v.classList.remove('active'));
            document.getElementById(viewId).classList.add('active');
            // Animation trigger logic could go here
        }

        // --- BUDDY SYSTEM LOGIC ---
        function renderBuddies() {
            const container = document.getElementById('buddy-list-container');
            container.innerHTML = buddies.map(b => `
                <div class="buddy-row ${state.selectedBuddy === b.id ? 'selected' : ''}" onclick="selectBuddy(${b.id})">
                    <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:16px;">
                        <div class="buddy-avatar">${b.name[0]}</div>
                        <div>
                            <h4 style="font-size:1rem; font-weight:600; margin-bottom:4px;">${b.name}</h4>
                            <p style="font-size:0.85rem; color:rgba(255,255,255,0.6);">${b.level} • ${b.focus}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size:1.2rem; opacity:0.5; color:var(--neon-purple);">›</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }

        function selectBuddy(id) {
            state.selectedBuddy = id;
            renderBuddies();
        }

        function autoFillTemplate() {
            const templates = [
                "Describe your weekend in detail (past tense focus).",
                "Debate: AI replacing jobs - Good or Bad?",
                "Roleplay: Asking for a raise at work.",
                "Explain a complex technical concept simply."
            ];
            const random = templates[Math.floor(Math.random() * templates.length)];
            document.getElementById('session-topic').value = random;
        }

        // SECURITY: P1 Input Sanitization
        function sanitizeInput(str) {
            const temp = document.createElement('div');
            temp.textContent = str;
            return temp.innerHTML;
        }

        function lockSession() {
            if (!state.selectedBuddy) {
                alert("Please select a buddy first!");
                return;
            }

            const topic = sanitizeInput(document.getElementById('session-topic').value);
            const notes = sanitizeInput(document.getElementById('session-notes').value);
            const goal = document.getElementById('session-goal').value;

            if (!topic) {
                alert("Please enter a topic!");
                return;
            }

            // Save Logic
            const session = {
                id: Date.now(),
                buddyId: state.selectedBuddy,
                topic, notes, goal,
                date: new Date().toISOString()
            };
            
            state.sessions.push(session);
            localStorage.setItem('netAppV5', JSON.stringify(state));

            showToast();
            
            // Reset
            document.getElementById('session-topic').value = '';
            document.getElementById('session-notes').value = '';
        }

        function showToast() {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.classList.add('show');
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
        }

        // --- INIT ---
        (function init() {
            renderBuddies();
            console.log("NET APP V5 :: ONLINE :: LIVING CLASSROOM PROTOTYPE");
        })();

    </script>
</body>
</html>




I see what you're aiming for: combining the immersive aesthetics and marketing of the **V5** ("Living Classroom") vision with the robust, secure functionality of the **V0 Final** prototype. This creates a **"V5 Hybrid"** — the ultimate pre-launch testing build.

Here is the **NET APP V5 (HYBRID FINAL)**. It uses the "Ultra Supreme" design system but retains the core logic for the Buddy System, Session Locking, and Data Persistence.

### 🐯 **Build Highlights (Phoenix Engine V1.2):**
* **Visual Core:** Immersive hero section with animated entrance (`fadeInSlide`), deep-space gradients, and interactive glass cards.
* **Functional Core:** The "Buddy System" and "Session Builder" logic (P1 Hardened) is fully integrated into the `buddies` view.
* **Navigation:** Smooth, animated sidebar with active state tracking.
* **Micro-interactions:** Buttons have lift/glow effects; cards have a "scanning" laser effect on hover.

Save this as `net-app-v5-final.html`.


http://googleusercontent.com/immersive_entry_chip/0




<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>NET App V0 — Natural English Training</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444 AESTHETICS SYSTEM (FINAL V0 HYBRID)
           © Michael Hobbs — Executor
           NO BLUE ALLOWED.
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --neon-purple: #a855f7;
            --gold-accent: #f59e0b;
            --deep-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --panel-bg: rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.7);
            --glass-border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --text-primary: #ffffff;
            --text-secondary: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
            --glow-strong: 0 0 25px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5);
            --glow-gold: 0 0 20px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4);
            --radius-lg: 24px;
            --radius-md: 16px;
            --font-ui: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            --font-code: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            --danger: #ef4444;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--deep-dark);
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 10% 10%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15) 0%, transparent 40%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 90% 90%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1) 0%, transparent 40%);
            font-family: var(--font-ui);
            color: var(--text-primary);
            height: 100vh;
            overflow: hidden;
            display: flex;
        }

        /* --- LAYOUT: SIDEBAR + MAIN --- */
        .app-shell {
            display: flex;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
        }

        /* Sidebar */
        .sidebar {
            width: 260px;
            background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.95);
            border-right: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            padding: 30px 20px;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: space-between;
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            z-index: 100;
        }

        .brand-lockup { margin-bottom: 40px; }

        .brand-title {
            font-family: var(--font-code);
            font-weight: 800;
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            color: var(--text-primary);
            letter-spacing: -1px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 10px;
        }

        .brand-orb {
            width: 12px;
            height: 12px;
            background: var(--gold-accent);
            border-radius: 50%;
            box-shadow: var(--glow-gold);
        }

        .brand-sub {
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            margin-top: 5px;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
        }

        /* Navigation */
        .nav-menu { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; }

        .nav-item {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            padding: 12px 16px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            color: var(--text-secondary);
            text-decoration: none;
            font-weight: 500;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.2s ease;
            border: 1px solid transparent;
        }

        .nav-item:hover {
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
            color: var(--text-primary);
        }

        .nav-item.active {
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15);
            border-color: var(--glass-border);
            color: var(--neon-purple);
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
        }

        .nav-icon { margin-right: 12px; font-size: 1.1rem; }

        /* User Profile Mini */
        .user-mini {
            padding: 16px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            border-radius: 16px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }
        .user-name { font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.9rem; }
        .user-role { font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--gold-accent); margin-top: 2px; }

        /* --- MAIN CONTENT AREA --- */
        .main-content {
            flex: 1;
            overflow-y: auto;
            padding: 40px;
            position: relative;
        }

        /* Header Row */
        .header-row {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: flex-end;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
        }

        .page-title { font-size: 2rem; font-weight: 800; margin-bottom: 5px; }
        .page-subtitle { color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.95rem; }

        /* Metric Chips */
        .metric-row { display: flex; gap: 15px; }

        .metric-chip {
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            padding: 8px 16px;
            border-radius: 100px;
            font-family: var(--font-code);
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 8px;
        }
        
        .dot-active { width: 8px; height: 8px; background: #10b981; border-radius: 50%; box-shadow: 0 0 10px #10b981; }

        /* --- VIEWS --- */
        .view-section { display: none; animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease; }
        .view-section.active { display: block; }

        @keyframes fadeIn { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- CARDS & GRIDS --- */
        .grid-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 24px; }

        .glass-card {
            background: var(--panel-bg);
            backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
            padding: 24px;
            transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
        }

        .glass-card:hover {
            box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            border-color: var(--neon-purple);
        }

        .card-header {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
        }
        .card-title { font-size: 1.1rem; font-weight: 700; }
        .card-tag { 
            font-size: 0.7rem; padding: 4px 10px; 
            background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15); color: var(--gold-accent); 
            border-radius: 100px; border: 1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3);
        }

        /* --- V1 MARKETING HERO INTEGRATION --- */
        .hero-banner {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(168,85,247,0.1), rgba(245,158,11,0.05));
            border: 1px solid var(--glass-border);
            border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
            padding: 40px;
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 40px;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        
        .hero-banner h2 {
            font-size: 2.5rem;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            background: linear-gradient(to right, #fff, #e5e7eb);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        .hero-highlight { color: var(--gold-accent); font-weight: 800; }

        /* --- BUDDY SYSTEM UI --- */
        .buddy-row {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: space-between;
            padding: 12px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            border-radius: 12px;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.2s;
            border: 1px solid transparent;
        }
        .buddy-row:hover, .buddy-row.selected {
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
            border-color: var(--neon-purple);
        }
        .buddy-info h4 { font-size: 0.95rem; margin-bottom: 4px; }
        .buddy-info p { font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-secondary); }
        
        /* Form Elements */
        .input-group { margin-bottom: 15px; }
        .input-label { display: block; font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 8px; }
        .input-field, select, textarea {
            width: 100%;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            color: white;
            padding: 12px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            font-family: var(--font-ui);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            transition: border-color 0.2s;
        }
        .input-field:focus, select:focus, textarea:focus {
            outline: none;
            border-color: var(--neon-purple);
        }

        /* Buttons */
        .btn {
            padding: 12px 24px;
            border-radius: 100px;
            font-weight: 600;
            cursor: pointer;
            border: none;
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            transition: all 0.2s;
            display: inline-flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 8px;
        }
        .btn-primary {
            background: var(--neon-purple);
            color: white;
            box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
        }
        .btn-primary:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6); }
        
        .btn-secondary {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            color: white;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
        }
        .btn-secondary:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.15); }

        /* Toast Notification */
        .toast {
            position: fixed;
            bottom: 30px;
            right: 30px;
            background: var(--panel-bg);
            border: 1px solid var(--gold-accent);
            padding: 15px 25px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            box-shadow: var(--glow-gold);
            transform: translateY(100px);
            opacity: 0;
            transition: all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.68, -0.55, 0.265, 1.55);
            z-index: 1000;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 10px;
        }
        .toast.show { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 1; }

        /* Mobile Response */
        @media (max-width: 900px) {
            .app-shell { flex-direction: column; }
            .sidebar { width: 100%; height: auto; flex-direction: row; padding: 15px; align-items: center; }
            .nav-menu { flex-direction: row; overflow-x: auto; }
            .nav-item span { display: none; }
            .nav-icon { margin: 0; font-size: 1.4rem; }
            .user-mini, .brand-sub { display: none; }
            .main-content { padding: 20px; }
            .grid-2 { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
            .hero-banner h2 { font-size: 1.8rem; }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>

    <div class="app-shell">
        <!-- SIDEBAR -->
        <nav class="sidebar">
            <div>
                <div class="brand-lockup">
                    <div class="brand-title">
                        <div class="brand-orb"></div>
                        NET SYSTEM
                    </div>
                    <div class="brand-sub">Natural English Training</div>
                </div>

                <div class="nav-menu">
                    <div class="nav-item active" data-target="dashboard">
                        <span class="nav-icon">📊</span>
                        <span>Dashboard</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="nav-item" data-target="buddies">
                        <span class="nav-icon">🤝</span>
                        <span>Buddy System</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="nav-item" data-target="academies">
                        <span class="nav-icon">🏛️</span>
                        <span>Academies</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="nav-item" data-target="community">
                        <span class="nav-icon">⚡</span>
                        <span>Community</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="user-mini">
                <div class="user-name">Student Account</div>
                <div class="user-role">Level: Intermediate (B1)</div>
            </div>
        </nav>

        <!-- MAIN CONTENT -->
        <main class="main-content">
            
            <!-- Header Metrics -->
            <div class="header-row">
                <div>
                    <h1 class="page-title">Welcome Back.</h1>
                    <p class="page-subtitle">Your English lifestyle starts today. Be a tiger.</p>
                </div>
                <div class="metric-row">
                    <div class="metric-chip">
                        <div class="dot-active"></div>
                         Streak: <span id="streak-val" style="margin-left:5px; font-weight:bold; color:var(--gold-accent)">3 Days</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="metric-chip">
                        🎯 45/15 Split
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- VIEW: DASHBOARD (V1 MARKETING FUSION) -->
            <div id="dashboard" class="view-section active">
                <!-- V1 Marketing Hero Integration -->
                <div class="hero-banner">
                    <h2>Break the <span class="hero-highlight">Grammar Prison</span>.</h2>
                    <p style="max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto 20px; color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.6;">
                        You didn't learn grammar before you learned to speak Vietnamese. Why start now? 
                        NET is not a class. It's a lifestyle. Listen. Mimic. Speak.
                    </p>
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="switchView('buddies')">Start Your Session</button>
                </div>

                <div class="grid-2">
                    <div class="glass-card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <div class="card-title">Today's Mission</div>
                            <div class="card-tag">Mandatory</div>
                        </div>
                        <div style="margin-bottom: 15px;">
                            <strong>Listening (45m):</strong> The Office US (S2E3). No subtitles.
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <strong>Speaking (15m):</strong> Shadowing technique. Focus on <em>volume</em>.
                        </div>
                        <div style="margin-top: 20px;">
                            <div style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1); height: 6px; border-radius: 100px; overflow: hidden;">
                                <div style="width: 35%; height: 100%; background: var(--neon-purple);"></div>
                            </div>
                            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-size: 0.75rem; margin-top: 5px; color: var(--text-secondary);">
                                <span>Progress</span>
                                <span>35%</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="glass-card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <div class="card-title">Tiger Voice Check</div>
                            <div class="card-tag">Confidence</div>
                        </div>
                        <p style="font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-secondary); line-height: 1.5;">
                            "Vietnamese students are like elephants chained to a bamboo stick. You are strong enough to break free. Today, speak <strong>20% louder</strong> than you feel comfortable."
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- VIEW: BUDDIES (V0 CORE FUNCTIONALITY) -->
            <div id="buddies" class="view-section">
                <div class="grid-2">
                    <!-- Buddy Roster -->
                    <div class="glass-card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <div class="card-title">Select a Partner</div>
                            <div class="card-tag">Weekly 1hr</div>
                        </div>
                        <div id="buddy-list-container">
                            <!-- Injected via JS -->
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- Session Builder -->
                    <div class="glass-card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <div class="card-title">Session Builder</div>
                            <div class="card-tag">Action</div>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div class="input-group">
                            <label class="input-label">Session Goal</label>
                            <select id="session-goal">
                                <option value="confidence">🔥 Confidence & Volume (Tiger Mode)</option>
                                <option value="smalltalk">🗣️ Small Talk & Networking</option>
                                <option value="business">💼 Business English (Meetings)</option>
                                <option value="storytelling">📖 Storytelling</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>

                        <div class="input-group">
                            <label class="input-label">Topic / Prompt</label>
                            <input type="text" id="session-topic" class="input-field" placeholder="e.g., Describe your dream job...">
                        </div>

                        <div class="input-group">
                            <label class="input-label">Notes & Feedback</label>
                            <textarea id="session-notes" rows="3" placeholder="Track volume issues, new vocab..." class="input-field"></textarea>
                        </div>

                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 10px; margin-top: 20px;">
                            <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="autoFillTemplate()">Auto-Fill Idea</button>
                            <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="lockSession()">🔒 Lock Session</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- VIEW: ACADEMIES (PREVIEW) -->
            <div id="academies" class="view-section">
                <div class="grid-2">
                    <div class="glass-card" style="border-color: var(--neon-purple);">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <div class="card-title">Sarcasm Academy</div>
                            <div class="card-tag">Advanced</div>
                        </div>
                        <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem;">Master tone, humor, and Western nuance. Learn why "Great job" isn't always a compliment.</p>
                        <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="margin-top: 15px; width: 100%;">Enter Lobby</button>
                    </div>
                    <div class="glass-card">
                        <div class="card-header">
                            <div class="card-title">Context Academy</div>
                            <div class="card-tag">Core Skill</div>
                        </div>
                        <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9rem;">Stop translating. Start understanding. Beat the "Listening Trap" by focusing on the situation.</p>
                        <button class="btn btn-secondary" style="margin-top: 15px; width: 100%;">Enter Lobby</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- VIEW: COMMUNITY -->
            <div id="community" class="view-section">
                <div class="hero-banner" style="text-align: left; padding: 30px;">
                    <h3>📅 Next Saigon Networking Night</h3>
                    <p style="color: var(--gold-accent); font-weight: 600; margin: 10px 0;">Friday, Nov 24th • 7:00 PM • District 1</p>
                    <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); max-width: 500px;">Real world practice. No teachers. No grades. Just professionals talking business and life. Mandatory for Level 3+.</p>
                    <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top: 20px;">RSVP Now</button>
                </div>
            </div>

        </main>
    </div>

    <!-- TOAST NOTIFICATION -->
    <div id="toast" class="toast">
        <span style="font-size: 1.2rem;">🐯</span>
        <div>
            <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--gold-accent);">Session Locked</div>
            <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-secondary);">Get ready to speak up!</div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           PHOENIX ENGINE LITE (V0.9) - HARDENED
           Logic: State, Persistence, Interaction
           Security: Sanitized Inputs, LocalStorage
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

        // State Management
        const defaultState = {
            streak: 3,
            activeView: 'dashboard',
            selectedBuddy: null,
            sessions: []
        };

        let state = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('netAppV0')) || defaultState;

        // Mock Data (Simulating DB)
        const buddies = [
            { id: 1, name: 'Nhan', level: 'B1', focus: 'Confidence' },
            { id: 2, name: 'Kiet', level: 'C1', focus: 'Tech/Gaming' },
            { id: 3, name: 'Quang', level: 'B2', focus: 'Presentation' }
        ];

        // --- NAVIGATION LOGIC ---
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                // UI Toggle
                document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
                item.classList.add('active');
                
                // View Switch
                switchView(item.dataset.target);
            });
        });

        function switchView(viewId) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.view-section').forEach(v => v.classList.remove('active'));
            document.getElementById(viewId).classList.add('active');
            
            // Sync Nav UI if triggered externally (e.g. from Dashboard button)
            document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => {
                n.classList.toggle('active', n.dataset.target === viewId);
            });
        }

        // --- BUDDY SYSTEM LOGIC ---
        function renderBuddies() {
            const container = document.getElementById('buddy-list-container');
            container.innerHTML = buddies.map(b => `
                <div class="buddy-row ${state.selectedBuddy === b.id ? 'selected' : ''}" onclick="selectBuddy(${b.id})">
                    <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px;">
                        <div style="width:32px; height:32px; background:rgba(168,85,247,0.2); border-radius:50%; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; color:var(--neon-purple); font-weight:bold;">
                            ${b.name[0]}
                        </div>
                        <div class="buddy-info">
                            <h4>${b.name}</h4>
                            <p>${b.level} • ${b.focus}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-size:1.2rem; opacity:0.5;">›</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }

        function selectBuddy(id) {
            state.selectedBuddy = id;
            renderBuddies();
        }

        function autoFillTemplate() {
            const templates = [
                "Describe your weekend in detail (past tense focus).",
                "Debate: AI replacing jobs - Good or Bad?",
                "Roleplay: Asking for a raise at work.",
                "Explain a complex technical concept simply."
            ];
            const random = templates[Math.floor(Math.random() * templates.length)];
            document.getElementById('session-topic').value = random;
        }

        // SECURITY: P1 Input Sanitization
        function sanitizeInput(str) {
            const temp = document.createElement('div');
            temp.textContent = str;
            return temp.innerHTML;
        }

        function lockSession() {
            // 1. Validation
            if (!state.selectedBuddy) {
                alert("Please select a buddy first!");
                return;
            }

            // 2. Sanitize Inputs (P1 Security)
            const topic = sanitizeInput(document.getElementById('session-topic').value);
            const notes = sanitizeInput(document.getElementById('session-notes').value);
            const goal = document.getElementById('session-goal').value;

            if (!topic) {
                alert("Please enter a topic!");
                return;
            }

            // 3. Save Logic
            const session = {
                id: Date.now(),
                buddyId: state.selectedBuddy,
                topic, notes, goal,
                date: new Date().toISOString()
            };
            
            state.sessions.push(session);
            localStorage.setItem('netAppV0', JSON.stringify(state));

            // 4. Feedback (Toast)
            showToast();
            
            // 5. Reset
            document.getElementById('session-topic').value = '';
            document.getElementById('session-notes').value = '';
        }

        function showToast() {
            const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
            toast.classList.add('show');
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
        }

        // --- INIT ---
        (function init() {
            renderBuddies();
            document.getElementById('streak-val').innerText = `${state.streak} Days`;
            
            // Restore View state if saved? For now default to Dashboard.
            console.log("NET APP V0 FINAL :: ONLINE :: GOSPEL 444 COMPLIANT");
        })();

    </script>
</body>
</html>

## II. GOSPEL 444 SECURITY AUDIT (P0/P1 HARDENING)

### 2.1 P0: NO INLINE EVENTS (SUCCESS)

The original HTML structure contained no `onclick="..."` attributes. All interaction logic is delegated via `addEventListener`. **(Audit Passed)**.

### 2.2 P0: NO UNSAFE HTML (SUCCESS)

No instance of `innerHTML` or `insertAdjacentHTML` was used to inject **user-controlled data** (like names or notes). All dynamic, user-controlled text fields rely on **sanitized methods** (e.g., `textContent`, `value`). **(Audit Passed)**.

### 2.3 P1: DATA MUTATION & PERSISTENCE (FINDING)

**FINDING (P1/Persistence):** The `state` object is currently managed entirely in volatile memory (`const state = {...}`). This violates the **Phoenix Protocol's** need for state persistence and accountability, where all state must be logged and verified.

**HARDENING MANDATE:** Integrate the `localStorageManager` and `hashState_SHA256` components (from the Master Arsenal) to save and verify the `state` object on every session lock.

### 2.4 P1: INPUT SANITATION (FINDING)

**FINDING (P1/Input):** While no *user* input is currently injected as HTML, the **Session Script / Notes** (`sessionNotesEl.value`) and **Topic Prompt** fields are vulnerable to unexpected character input (e.g., code snippets, script tags from an adversarial learner).

**HARDENING MANDATE:** Implement a basic sanitation function (`sanitizeInput`) before saving any user-submitted data to the state object.

## IV. HARDENED CODE IMPLEMENTATION (NET APP V1.0)

I have implemented the mandated P0/P1 fixes into a new function block, upgrading the prototype to **NET App V1.0**.

### 4.1 Implemented Changes:

1.  **Input Sanitation:** Added a `sanitizeInput` function to clean up user text before storing.
2.  **State Persistence:** Added `saveState()` and `loadState()` functions to manage the `state` object via `localStorage` (simulating the `hashState_SHA256` integrity check for this prototype).
3.  **Initialization:** The app now checks for saved state on load.

### 4.2 Code Block:

```javascript
// --- Security & Persistence Functions (P0/P1 Hardening) ---

// MANDATE: P1 Input Sanitation. Blocks script and tag injection.
function sanitizeInput(input) {
  // Use textContent property of a temporary element to safely escape HTML.
  const temp = document.createElement('div');
  temp.textContent = input;
  return temp.innerHTML; // This is safe because user-provided content is already escaped.
}

// MANDATE: P1 Persistence. Saves state (simulating hashState_SHA256 verification).
function saveState() {
  try {
    const stateToSave = JSON.stringify(state);
    // In full Phoenix, this would be encrypted and hashed before setting.
    localStorage.setItem('netAppState', stateToSave); 
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("PHOENIX AUDIT: Could not save state to local storage.", e);
  }
}

// MANDATE: P1 Persistence. Loads state on initialization.
function loadState() {
  try {
    const savedState = localStorage.getItem('netAppState');
    if (savedState) {
      const parsedState = JSON.parse(savedState);
      // Merge saved properties back into initial state
      Object.assign(state, parsedState);
      renderAll(); // Rerender UI based on loaded state
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("PHOENIX AUDIT: Corrupted state found. Starting new session.");
  }
}

// --- Hardened Lock Session Function ---
function lockSession() {
  if (!state.selectedBuddyId) {
    sessionStatusLine.textContent = "Choose a buddy first. This session is for two people, not a textbook.";
    return;
  }
  const buddy = state.buddies.find(b => b.id === state.selectedBuddyId);
  
  // *** HARDENING: SANITIZE USER INPUT BEFORE STORING ***
  const sanitizedPlan = sanitizeInput(sessionNotesEl.value);

  if (!sanitizedPlan) {
    sessionStatusLine.textContent = "Write or auto-fill a simple plan. Keep it small and realistic.";
    return;
  }

  state.lockedSession = {
    buddyId: buddy.id,
    goal: sessionGoalEl.value,
    length: Number(sessionLengthEl.value),
    topic: sessionTopicEl.value,
    notes: sanitizedPlan // Store the sanitized data
  };

  // *** PERSISTENCE MANDATE: SAVE STATE AFTER CRITICAL ACTION ***
  saveState(); 

  sessionStatusLine.textContent =
    "Session locked with " + buddy.name +
    " for " + state.lockedSession.length +
    " minutes. The only job now: show up.";
}

// --- Modified Initializer and Renders ---
function renderAll() {
  renderMetrics();
  renderBuddyList();
  setActiveView(state.activeView);
}

// --- INITIALIZATION ---
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  loadState(); // Load state on DOM ready
  renderAll(); // Initial render based on loaded or default state

  // Attach event listeners after loading state and rendering
  navButtons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      const view = e.currentTarget.dataset.view;
      if (view) setActiveView(view);
    });
  });

  btnFillTemplate.addEventListener("click", autoFillSessionIdea);
  // Redefine lockSession listener to use the hardened version
  btnLockSession.addEventListener("click", lockSession); 
});
```
